# vmu 14 · 未决、风险与路线图

## 0. docs 审判口径与量化结论（轮 53–56）

> **背景** ✓：打磨阶段产生了**极多条目**（23 卷 ✓；**行数与码数不再手写** ✓ —— 都可重算 ✓）⇒ 需要判断"**哪些内容真的没必要做**" ✓。本节的**口径与结论**是其后所有裁剪的依据 ✓，**不允许凭感觉砍** ✗。

### 0.1 五问口径（**任一"是" ⇒ 留；五问全"否" ⇒ 判"不做 ✗"**）

**⓪ 前提一步（轮 61 加，**先查证再裁决** ✗✓）**：**这个条目是否已经以别的名字实现了？** —— **在判"做/不做"之前，必须先 `grep` 实现面** ✓✓。
> 加这一步的原因是一次**实测教训** ✓：轮 61 准备为"**设备台账/预约/校准/维护**"写新模块 ✗，`grep` 后发现**该族早已由 `kernel/instruments.js`（14/17 键 ✓，含 `blockOnOverdue`／`reserve`／`waitlistPolicy`／`scheduleMaintenance`／`attachCapture`）＋ `kernel/capacity.js`（`facilities`＋重叠拒 ✓）实现** ✓✓ ⇒ **"做 ✓"若照原样落地，就是白写一整套重复面** ✗。
> **同源教训（轮 59）** ✓：我曾判"协议闸**不做 ✗**"，理由是"**已由 `storepolicy`／`compliance` 承载**" ✗ —— **grep 后发现根本没有承载面** ✗✓，只有 `ip` 的一条未签拒 ⇒ **改判"做 ✓"并已落地** ✓。
> ⇒ **两次错误方向相反，根因相同：不核就用** ✗。**故 ⓪ 是硬前提** ✓。

① 是否**改变某个决定**（代理或人实际会做的取舍）？② 是否**可经 settings／中间件／pack 调控**？③ 是否**有消费者**（代码读点，或有明确归属＋已登记键/码）？④ 去掉**是否真的会丢能力**？⑤ 是否**与别处重复**？
**处置** ✓：判"不做 ✗"者**只留一行＋可核理由**（**不删历史、不重复展开** ✗✓），并写明**改判条件**（**出现真实消费者 ⇒ 恢复为规格** ✓）。

### 0.2 机械判据（"源 vs 复制"）

| 标记 | 含义 | 处置 |
|---|---|---|
| 标题写"**登记进 `03-§8`**" | **声明源** ✓ | **保留**（生成管线以此为输入） |
| 标题写"**由 `03-§8` 唯一登记**" | **复制品** ✗ | **砍成一行指针** ✓ |
| 正文与**本卷裁决表**矛盾 | **内部不一致** ✗ | **让散文服从裁决** ✓ |
| 卷内写明"**不重复登记**"（如 17-§、08-§、21-§） | **前轮已治理** ✓ | **保留并作为范式** ✓ |

### 0.3 量化结论（**实测，非估计** ✓）

- **实现里不存在的码 = 474 / 1164（41%）** ✓ —— 但它们**集中在 `03-§8` 登记表**（**那是登记处本身，合法** ✓）；**非登记卷的重复展开**才是膨胀 ✗。
- **可疑"计划规格"占比很小** ✓：`08` = 5 节 / 95 行（**7%**）；`17` = 4 节 / 97 行（**11%**）⇒ **这两卷的体量主要是"键表＋一行式设计条目"**（**密度已到极值、每行都带决策与四哲学检查** ✓）⇒ **再砍即破坏设计资产** ✗✓。
- **已执行的裁剪（3 处 ＋ 1 项补登记）** ✓：`22-§14` 90 码复制表（94 行 ⇒ 1 行指针 ＋ 7 行裁决 ✓）；`07-§4.11` 知识图谱面（44 行 ⇒ 9 行裁决 ✓）；`22-§3.0` 货币类型（106 行 ⇒ 一行标题 ✓）⇒ **卷 22：1101 → 922 行、卷 07：751 → 712 行** ✓。
- ★ **轮 67–68 实测：270 个"已接线"的键不在核心 schema 里** ✗✓✓ —— **12 个内核模块**导出 `WIRED_KEYS`（`capacity` 12／`compliance` 16／`conference` 18／`external` 27／`funding` 16／`hr` 11／`ip` 20／`mathtools` 45／`meetings` 47／`migration` 11／`records` 24／`workflow` 23 ＝ **270** ✓），而 `settings/schema.js`（**core，422 条** ✓）**一条都没有** ✗；**`planned.js` 一直在替它们遮掩** ✓✓（把**被真实读取**的键标成"**尚未实现**" ⇒ **门禁量到的是标签，不是真相** ✗）。
  **实测细节** ✓：① 试做"生成器按 `WIRED_KEYS` 排除 planned" ⇒ `planned 820 → 565`、`wired 246` ✓，**但三门禁当场变红** ✗✓（`audit-vmu-docs`／`vmu-settings` 都断言"**每个键都在 `schema.js`**" ⇒ 它们**既不在 core 也不在 planned** ⇒ **凭空消失** ✗）⇒ **已回滚保绿** ✓，**真问题暴露** ✓；② **默认值可机械取回 214/270（79%）** ✓（按 `sget('k', <literal>)`／`readKey`／`b()`／`n()`／对象字面量 ✓ —— **不猜** ✗）；**56 个不可取回** ✗（`compliance` 16／`hr` 11／`records` 24／`ip` 5 ⇒ 用**另一种取值写法** ⇒ **需先补提取规则** ✓）。
  **计划（两步必须同行 ✓）** ✓：① **把这 270 条迁入 `settings/schema.js`（core）** ✓✓ —— **必须内联**（`vmu-board-audit`／`vmu-bidding-audit` **按文本**读 `schema.js` ✗ ⇒ 拆文件会破坏它们 ✓）；`def` **取自模块自身**（**不猜** ✗），取不回者 `def: null` ＋ `doc` **明写"默认值未取回"** ✗；② **然后**生成器的 `wired` 排除才成立 ✓（**只做①会露出空洞，只做②会像本轮一样变红** ✓）。
  **★ 结局（轮 69–79，已落地并验证 ✓✓）**：**270 → 241 条迁入 core**（**24 条本已在 schema**（`records` 的 `EXTRA_WIRED_KEYS` ✓）／**5 条是"服务面"而非旋钮**（`ip` 自述"20 ＝ 15 旋钮 ＋ 5 服务面" ✓）⇒ **有意排除并写明理由** ✓）；**默认值取回 240/241（99.6%）** ✓✓（规则全部**从源码读出**：`sget`／`raw(settings,'k',<lit>)`／`b()`／`n()`／对象字面量／`[K.alias]`／比较式（`!== false`⇒true ✓）／包装式（`str()`⇒'' ✓）／单参 `listOr`⇒`[]` ✓／**位移与两操作数算术（手工求值，绝不用 `eval`** ✗）／数字分隔符／科学计数法 ✓）；**余 1 条明写"未取回"** ✗（跨行嵌套三元 ⇒ 规则待写 ✓）；**生成链 8/8** ✓（新步**必须排在 planned 之前** ✓，因为该注册表的 `coreKeys()` 读的就是 schema ✓）；**T1 114/114** ✓✓；**四条不变量已进生成链** ✓（不得重复声明／不得与手写 schema 撞键／**未取回的默认值必须在文件里说出来** ✓／**模块读取的键不得被静默丢弃** ✓）。

| 类别 | 原 v5r | **v5 预设**（`vibe-math-v5.js` ✓） | **vmu 全树**（kernel+packs+middleware+settings 30,072 行） |

| **`【…】` 提示段落** | 18 | **17（94%）** ✓✓ | **0（0%）** ✗ |
| 码名 | 47 | **46（98%）** ✓ | **1（2%）** ✗ |
| 阶段 phase | 10 | **8（80%）** ✓ | **0（0%）** ✗ |

  **确证缺口** ✗✓（**逐条 grep 核过** ✓，非提取伪影 ✓）：**9 个工具** —— `chair_proxy`／`motion`／`procedural`／`poll_open`／`poll_vote`／`poll_close`／`minutes`／`grant`／`end_verify`（**在 v5 预设里 0 命中** ✗）；**1 段提示**（`资源` ✓）；**1 个码**（`V5_RESOURCE_BUDGET` 只在 v5r 出现 4 处、v5 预设里没有 ✓）；**2 个阶段**（`closed`／`open` ✓）。
  ⇒ ★ **规律** ✓✓：**缺的正是"会议治理族"**（主持代理／动议／程序性／表决开票计票／纪要／经费授予／验证收束 ✓）—— **而这一族恰好是 vmu 的 `08`（会议/表决/工作流原语）与 `17`（社会机制）两卷所规划的面** ✓✓ ⇒ **两条线在此交汇** ✓：**v5r 缺的、vmu 规划了、但 vmu 尚未实现（仍在 `planned` 的 524 条里** ✗）。
  **结论（对"v5r 整合包是否完整"的回答）** ✗✓：**不完整，且差距是结构性的** —— `packs/v5r/prompts/v5r-core.js` **仅 122 行**（**策略包：码 ＋ 一条策略钩子** ✓，**0/56 工具、0/18 提示** ✗）；**v5r 的逻辑与提示词在 `vibe-math-v5r/`（原线）与 `vibe-math-v5/`（后继预设）里** ✓；**目前没有任何门禁在核对这条对齐** ✗（现有 `audit-*-parity` 都不是 v5r 对齐 ✓）⇒ **本轮给出的是可复现的度量，而非"完整"的断言** ✓✓。

  | v5r 工具 | vmu 框架是否已有承载 | 证据 |

  ⇒ **判据层面的修正** ✓✓：**"v5 预设缺的工具名" ≠ "vmu 缺的能力"** ✗ —— **框架给原语、预设给线**（vmu 理念 ✓）⇒ **parity 必须按【能力】核对，而不是按【工具名】计数** ✓✓；**真正缺失的只有"显式结束辩论"**（1 项 ✓），**`grant` 未证**（需按 ⓪ 步继续核 ✓）。

  ★ **轮 86 补核（⓪ 步做完 ✓）**：**`grant` 在 vmu 里也没有对应原语** ✗✓（kernel 里只有弱信号：`usedAt` 11 处多为**幂等台账** ✓、`one-shot` 2 处 ✓、`临时`/`授权` 各 1 处 ⇒ **均非"范围化单命令授权"** ✗）。**v5r 的语义已逐字读到** ✓✓：`GRANTABLE_COMMANDS = ['assign','prioritize','nudge','convene']`（**枚举命令** ✓）＋ `GRANT_SCOPES = ['meeting','verify','once']`（**窗口** ✓）＋ 字段 `grantScope`／`expiresOn`／`usedAt`／`expiredAt`／`why`／`active` ✓；**仅院士可授** ✓；**目标必须是在册成员**（**D8：非成员 ⇒ 先具名拒、后写台账** ✓✓）。
  ⇒ **两项确缺，且都通过五问** ✓✓：
  | 缺项 | ①改决定 | ②可调控 | ③消费者 | ④丢能力 | ⑤重复 | 裁决 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | **`end_verify`**（显式结束辩论，使聚合可运行） | ✓ | ✓ | **✓✓ 实测：`tests/selfdrive-v5.mjs:2836` 真的调用 `vibe_v5_end_verify`** | ✓ | ✗ | **做 ✓** |
  | **`grant`**（临时范围化单命令授权） | ✓ | ✓ | **✓（v5r 与 v5 预设线都用）** | ✓ | ✗ | **做 ✓** |
  **落地位置（按 vmu 理念 ✓）** ✓：**两项都进【框架】，不进预设** —— `end_verify` 属**收束/聚合闸**（与 `meeting` 的 `roundComplete`／`close` 同族 ✓）；`grant` 属**授权面**（与 `domaingate`／`storepolicy` 的"闸"同族 ✓，**枚举命令 ＋ 窗口 ＋ 一次性 ＋ 具名留痕** ✓）。**未做 ✗ 明写**：两者**尚未实现** ✓。




- ★★ **轮 92：524 个计划键的"消费者"问题，用两种扫描给出一致答案** ✓✓（**方法：先扫 7 种字面取值形态，再扫"作为字符串字面量出现"的更宽形态** ✓）：
  | 扫描 | 结果 |
  |---|---|
  | 7 种取值形态（`sget`／`raw`／`readKey`／`b()`／`n()`／`settings['k']`／`settings["k"]`） | **"读了却标 planned" = 0** ✓✓（**轮 81 的结论在更宽扫描下依然成立** ✓） |
  | **字符串字面量**（`'vmu.x.y'`，kernel 全目录） | **761 个键被点名** ✓；**"仍标 planned" = 0** ✓✓；**"两边都没登记" = 1** ⇒ **经查是假阳性** ✓✓ |
  **那 1 个假阳性值得记下** ✓✓：`vmu.settings.writers` 出现在 `kernel/index.js` ✓，但代码是 **`const WRITERS = Symbol('vmu.settings.writers')`** ✓，且注释明写"**记录存在 SYMBOL 键下，因此永不出现在 `Object.keys`、永不虚增声明键计数、永不泄漏进 pack 的 settings 层**" ✓✓ ⇒ **它是【故意】不是设置** ✗ ⇒ **扫描必须会读代码的意图，而不只是读字符串** ✓（**本族第 26 例：我自己扫描器的假阳性** ✓）。
- ★ **轮 94：第二批命名空间裁决（6 个面、67 键 ＝ 全表 13%）** ✓✓（**⓪ 步逐面做完** ✓）：
  | 命名空间 | 键 | ⓪ 已有载体？ | 五问 | 裁决 |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | **`vmu.notify`** | 15 | ★ **有** —— **`kernel/notify.js` 真的读 `vmu.notify.*`（9 处）** ✓✓ | ①✓②✓③**部分有**✓④✓⑤✗ | **保留为待办 ✗（不是空规划）** —— **9 键已接线、15 键仍 planned** ✓ ⇒ **需逐键核**（下一轮 ✓） |
  | **`vmu.funds`** | 11 | **无** ✗（卷 22 已判**不做**：vmu 的金额是**预算约束 ＋ 审计事实**，**不是会计系统** ✓） | ①✓②✓③✗④✗⑤✗ | **不做 ✗**（**复述并沿用既有裁决** ✓） |
  | **`vmu.dissemination`** | 11 | **部分有** ✓（**证据保真已落地**：论文轨道交付点 **`evidence` 为空 ⇒ 具名拒 ＋ 有界再问** ✓✓） | ①✓②✓③✗④✗⑤✗ | **其余不做 ✗**；**`evidenceFidelity` 一项已实现** ✓ |
  | **`vmu.collab`** | 10 | **无** ✗（跨机构协作：协议/署名/数据共享/联邦对等 ✓） | ①✓②✓③✗④✗⑤✗ | **不做 ✗**（**复活条件**：真出现跨机构协作需求时 ✓） |

  | **`vmu.roles`** | 10 | ★ **经查不是重复** ✓✓ —— **`hr.js` 的 8 个相关关键词全部 0 命中** ✗，其 11 键是**招聘/任期/绩效/申诉/离职** ✓；**任期/晋升/分离/代理/罢免**是**另一项能力** ✓ | ①✓②✓③✗④✗⑤✗ | **不做 ✗**（**但⓪步有实值**：**它证明了这不是重复，而是真空白** ✓ ⇒ **复活条件**：研究院真要形式化"角色任期/代理"时 ✓） |
  ⇒ **方法学收获** ✓✓：**⓪ 步（找已有载体）能区分三种完全不同的情况** —— **① 已被别的名字承载 ⇒ 合并** ⇄（本轮未出现 ✓）／**② 同名载体存在且真的在读 ⇒ 不是空规划** ✓（`notify` ✓）／**③ 名字相近但语义不同 ⇒ 真空白** ✓（`roles` vs `hr` ✓）—— **若跳过 ⓪ 步，这三者会被同一句"没有消费者"抹平** ✗✓。

  ⇒ **由此得到一条普遍事实** ✓✓：**524 个计划键中，没有任何一个在 kernel 里被字符串点名** ✓ ⇒ **整张计划表在框架内【零消费者】** ✓；并且 **v5 预设提及 `vmu.*` = 0 处、取值调用 = 0** ✓ ⇒ **它也不读框架设置** ✓。
- ★ **第一个命名空间的裁决（`vmu.math`，126 键 ＝ 全表 24%）** ✓✓：它是 **25 个数学子域的开关/后端** ✓（`stats` 13／`discrete` 12／`nt` 12／`optim` 12／`linalg` 10／`ode` 10／`geom` 9／`symbolic` 9／`formal` 8／`bayes` 6／`units` 5 …，每个子域含基键 ＋ `backend` ＋ 细节 ✓）。**五问结果** ✓：①改变决定 ✓（开关/后端 ✓）②可调控 ✓ ③**消费者 ✗（实测零字符串引用** ✓）④去掉不丢能力 ✗（**引擎在 `vibe-math-v5/math-computation.js` 与 `math-engines.js`** ✓，不读这些键 ✓）⑤重复 ✗ ⇒ **判：不作实现 ✗，但【保留为已声明的扩展面】** ✓✓ —— **理由**：它们**正是本目标 ① 所要求的"尽可能广的扩展方向"** ✓，而 `planned.js` **就是承载扩展面的机制** ✓；**复活条件** ✓：**当某个数学面真的要在框架层暴露可调开关时**（届时按 ⓪ 步先找已有载体 ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 105：`vmu.scheduler.*` 与 `vmu.schedule.*` 是"一个旋钮、两个名字"，而裁决只执行了五分之一** ✗✓✓（**本族第 37 例，且是两个生成物互相矛盾** ✓）：
  **证据** ✓：`kernel/scheduler.js:47-54` 的注释原文 —— **"INTEGRATOR RULING：`vmu.schedule.*` 是规范族（docs/08 §12.6），此别名指向规范名。读一个【未声明的孪生名】会迫使门禁为同一个旋钮要求第二份声明。"** ✓✓ —— **而实测五个别名里只有一个执行了** ✗：

  const A_MAX_PENDING  = 'scheduler.maxTriggers'          ← 仍是孪生名 ✗（此处不写 vmu. 前缀，以免被生成器当成声明源 ✗✓）
  const A_TIME_SOURCE  = 'scheduler.timeSource'           ← 仍是孪生名 ✗
  const A_ACTIONS      = 'scheduler.actionsAllowed'       ← 仍是孪生名 ✗
  const A_SEEDS        = 'scheduler.triggers'             ← 仍是孪生名 ✗

- ★★★ **轮 133：生成器两版对比【实测结案】＋ 修好一处真实缺陷（恢复数 287→290）** ✓✓
  **① 对比结案（有实测，不再是猜测 ✓）**：用 `git worktree` 取出 `1927e22` 的生成器（**字节忠实**，`git hash-object` 与 git blob **一致** ✓），放回本树**只报告**地跑一次 ⇒ **theirs：发射 245 键／恢复 237／缺 8**；**mine：发射 298 键／恢复 287／缺 11** ✓。逐键比对（D 盘脚本 ✓）⇒ ★ **theirs 的键集（422）是本树键集（720）的真子集** ✓，`keys only theirs emits = 0` ✓，**可移植项 = 0** ✓✓ ⇒ **未决项关闭：theirs 的 `defaultOf` 在【当前树】上没有 mine 缺的规则** ✓。
  **② 但实测暴露了我方一处真实缺陷** ✗：mine 的 `NO-DEFAULT` 里有 `course.submitMode`／`visibility`／`ontologyVersion` —— **它们的默认值明明写在源码里**（`s('vmu.course.submitMode', 'artifact')` ✓），**只是传给了模块【自己定义的短助手】** ✗。原实现的白名单写作 `(?:sget|b|n)` ✗ —— **又是一个"清单即字面量"** ✓（第 9 次同族 ✓）。**修法（按最优实践，不是补一个字母 ✓）**：把规则**改写成性质** —— **任何【模块自己定义的、参数 ≥ 2 的】函数**，只要**第一参数是键字面量、第二参数是字面量**，该字面量就是"缺省时的答案" ✓（**可从源码证明，非猜测** ✓；已知访问器**优先** ✓；**单参助手永不匹配** ✓）。**实测结果**：**`recovered 287 → 290`**、**`missing 11 → 8`** ✓✓、`INVARIANTS ok … 8 with an honest null default` ✓。
  **③ ★ 第 8 次"我的仪器错了"，而这次【在断言之前】被拦住** ✓✓：我先前用 PowerShell `>` 把 theirs 导出到 D 盘 ⇒ 它写成 **UTF-16**（**12006 字节的 blob 变成 24236 字节** ✓）⇒ 运行报 `SyntaxError` ✗ ⇒ **若我据此宣布"theirs 坏了"，就是又一次拿坏仪器当证据** ✗。**拦住它的办法是哈希**：`git hash-object 提取物` ≠ `git rev-parse <commit>:<path>` ⇒ **提取不忠实** ✓✓ ⇒ 改用 `git worktree` ＋ 文件复制 ✓。**并入纪律：跨提交/跨版本取文件，必须先以哈希证明提取忠实** ✓。
  **④ 明写未做 ✗**：**仍有 8 个 null**（`auction.dirtyWorkQuota`／`fairnessPolicy`／`rotationWindow`／`course.latePenaltyRatio`／`peerWeight`／`external.mergePolicy`／`grant.commands`／`instruments.capabilityTags` ✓）—— 其中 `auction` 三个的默认值是 **`DEFAULTS.<field>`（标识符）** ✗ ⇒ 需**从模块自身的 `DEFAULTS` 对象字面量**解析 ✓（**复活条件**：按"可证明"原则加一条 `IDENT.field` 解析规则并重跑链与 T1 ✓）；其余多**确实没有默认值**（`Number(readKey(...))` ✓／`listOr(sget(...))` ✓）⇒ **诚实 null** ✓。

  **④ 明写未做 ✗（已被轮 134 收窄）**：当时**仍有 8 个 null** ⇒ **轮 134 降到 3** ✓（见下 ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 134：把"默认值恢复"从【形态清单】改写成【可证明的性质】，恢复数 287 → 295** ✓✓
  **三处同族缺陷，都是"清单/单形态代替性质"** ✗✓：
  **① 助手白名单**（轮 133 已修 ✓）：`(?:sget|b|n)` ✗ ⇒ 改写成**"模块自己定义的、参数 ≥ 2 的函数，第一参数是键字面量、第二参数是字面量"** ✓。

  **实测（逐步）** ✓：`287 → 290`（①）→ `292`（②）→ **`295`（③）**；**`missing 11 → 3`** ✓✓；`INVARIANTS ok … 3 with an honest null default` ✓。**抽查确认非臆造** ✓：`fairnessPolicy='equal'` ✓／`dirtyWorkQuota=0` ✓／`submitMode='artifact'` ✓／`visibility='cohort'` ✓／`peerWeight=0` ✓（**全部与源码字面一致** ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 142：把 A/B 表里那个"已规格化的缺口"补成能力，并**刻意做得比对面好** ✓✓
  **背景** ✓：轮 141 的 A/B 表指出 vmu **发布了 9 个工具名却缺"消费它们的净化器"** ✗（缺口已被**实测规格化** ✓）。本轮补上 ✓。
  **实现** ✓：`host.js` 新增 **`sanitizeToolFilter(filter, known = TOOL_NAMES)`** ✓ —— 接受数组／逗号串／缺省 ✓，`known` 接受 `Set`／数组／`TOOL_NAMES` 对象 ✓。
  ★ **刻意优于 v5r 的一点** ✓✓：v5r 遇**未知名 ⇒ 整表变 `undefined`** ✗（**一处笔误就让过滤静默失效** ✗✓）；vmu ⇒ **具名拒 `VMU_INVALID_ARGUMENT`**，且 **`err.unknown` 点名未知项**、`hint` 给出**已知面** ✓✓（**"悄悄不再过滤"比"说我没听懂"更糟** ✓）。
  **另有三处刻意的严格性** ✓：① **丢弃必须计数**（`dropped: {empty, duplicate}` ✓，实测 `{empty:2, duplicate:1}` ✓）；② **`null`（无限制）与 `[]`（显式为空）不混同** ✓（v5r 混同 ✗）；③ **已知面为【空】时无法核查 ⇒ 透传并自曝 `checked:false`** ✓（**"未检查"也必须说出来** ✓）。
  **A/B 表随之更新** ✓✓：`sanitizeToolFilter` **从"未配对"移入"分歧"** ✓（H1–H9 **两侧都驱动** ✓：同一输入 ⇒ v5r `undefined` ✓ vs vmu 具名拒 ✓）⇒ **UNPAIRED 5 → 4** ✓、**DIVERGENT 3 → 4** ✓、A/B 套件 **37 → 46 条全绿** ✓。
  ★ **并把"自相矛盾"当场修掉** ✓：缺口补上后，我一度让同一条**同时挂在"已配对"和"未配对"**两边 ✗ ⇒ **已删掉未配对那条** ✓（本项目专门抓这种自相矛盾 ✓）。
  **过程失误（记下 ✓）** ✗：本轮我的编辑两次把**块的收尾 `}` 用掉**，语法检查两次当场报错（`Unexpected token ')'` ✗／`Unexpected end of input` ✗）⇒ **"先 `node --check` 再跑"这条纪律每次都救了我** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 143：把"可测性缺口"正面修好 —— 夹取从"不可观测"变成【已驱动】** ✓✓
  **背景** ✓：轮 140 的 A/B 表把 `clamp01` 记为 **TESTABILITY GAP**（不是"没有对应物" ✓）：`allocation()` 把 `reserveRatio` 夹进 [0,1] ✓，但**没有任何地方暴露结果** ✗ —— `reserveRatio: 5` 与 `0.5` 产生**完全相同的 `status()`** 且**完全相同的分配输出** ✗ ⇒ **看不见效果的旋钮，等于没人能核对的旋钮** ✓。
  **修法（正面补可观测性，而不是把它写成"做不到" ✓）**：`fairness.js` 新增 **`effectiveKnobs()`** ✓，并在 **`status().effective`** 回显**每个被夹取旋钮的【声明值 vs 生效值】** ✓ 并**点名哪些被调整过**（`adjusted: []` ✓）。**实测** ✓：`5 ⇒ {declared:5, effective:1} adjusted=['reserveRatio']` ✓／`-3 ⇒ {declared:-3, effective:0}` ✓／`0.25 ⇒ 原样且 adjusted=[]` ✓／`'abc' ⇒ {declared:"abc", effective:0} adjusted` ✓（**非法声明值也被点名** ✓）／**缺省 ⇒ 不算被调整** ✓。
  **A/B 表随之升级** ✓✓：新增 **I1–I6**（**两侧都驱动** ✓）⇒ 该行从"无法 A/B"变成 **PROPERTY（同性质：越界有限值夹取 ✓、域内原样 ✓）＋ DIVERGENT（非有限值：v5r 取中点 0.5 vs vmu 回落到声明默认 0 ✓）** ✓⇒ A/B 套件 **46 → 52 条全绿** ✓、**PROPERTY 5 → 6** ✓。
  **★ 并当场删掉重复与矛盾** ✓：旧的"非有限值"分歧条目被 **I6 取代** ⇒ 删掉旧的 ✓（同一件事不能同时挂在"已驱动"与"仅记录"两边 ✗）。
  **过程教训（记下 ✓）** ✗：我第一版探针按别的面的 **`{ get(k) }`** 形状喂 settings ✗ ⇒ **三个断言全红** ✓；实测记录到 **`requested keys = []`** ⇒ 才查明 `fairness.js` 的访问器是 **`read(s,k,d)` 用 `hasOwnProperty` 直读普通对象**（并回落 `s.fairness`/`s.budget`/`s` 的短名 ✓）⇒ ★ **框架里 settings 不止一种形状** ✓✓，**必须按模块实测，不能按记忆套用** ✓。

- ★★ **轮 145：回到"框架自身长尾"——把 `vmu.paper.maxSections` 从"声明未接"变成【真限制】** ✓✓
  **做法** ✓：`paper.js` 把该键**移入 `WIRED_KEYS`** ✓、在 `cfg()` 里读 ✓、并在 **`start()`** 里执行 ✓：**超限即具名拒 `VMU_RESOURCE_BUDGET`**，**消息同时给实际数与上限** ✓（`'too many sections: 3 > 2'` ✓），**默认 `0`＝不限 ⇒ 既有行为零变化** ✓。
  **并定下【检查顺序】** ✓：**尺寸拒绝优先于归属拒绝** ✓ —— 一篇 100 节的稿件应当因**体量**被拒，而不是因**一长串无主章节** ✓（已断言 ✓）。
  **测试** ✓：套件 **30 → 37 条全绿** ✓（M1 默认不限 ✓／M2 超限具名拒 ✓／**M3 消息含 "3 > 2"** ✓／M4 恰好等于上限**放行**（界限是 `>` 不是 `>=` ✓）／**M5 尺寸优先** ✓／M6-M7 键表自述与实际一致 ✓）。
  ★ **并在生成器里修掉一个**同一族的缺陷 ✓：`Number(readKey('k') || 0)` 的捕获**多带一个 `)`** ⇒ 该键被写成 **NO-DEFAULT（诚实 null）** ✗ —— 与轮 134 那次同类 ✓。**修法按同一原则**：**仅在"去掉尾部括号后仍是通过字面量判定"时才接受** ✓（`bar(1))`／`getDefault())` 仍被拒 ✓ ⇒ **不放宽判定** ✓。**实测**：**`TOTAL keys 300 → 301`** ✓、**`recovered 297 → 298`** ✓、**诚实 null 仍为 3** ✓（**没有把问题搬成新的 null** ✓）。
  **docs 同步** ✓：生成的 `04-§11` 自动标为 **✅ 已接线** ✓；我手写的 `08` 那行**已过时**（仍写着"未接 2 个" ✗）⇒ **改为"可调 3 个／未接 1 个"** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 146：仪器面三键"移回已接线"＋`priorityPolicy` 从"只记录"变成【真判定】** ✓✓
  **发现（又一次"理由把机制说错了" ✗，一次三例）** ✓✓：`instruments.js` 的计划表把 **`priorityPolicy`／`waitlistPolicy`／`hashAlgo`** 记为"**未读取该键**" ✗ —— 而实测**三个都被读取** ✓：`cfg()` 读它们 ✓；★ **`fp()` 用了 `cfg().hashAlgo` 作为指纹前缀** ✓（**它的理由"本面不做指纹计算"完全错了** ✗）；`reserve()` 用 `priorityPolicy` 决定/记录、用 `waitlistPolicy` 决定 `waitlist` 字段 ✓。⇒ **与轮 120 的 `course.visibility` 同类** ✓（**对效果说对一半、对机制说错** ✗）。
  **处置** ✓：三者**移回 `WIRED_INSTRUMENT_KEYS`** ✓，并在原位写下**如实更正**（"轮 115 的理由对机制是错的" ✓），**保留**的只有关于 `priorityPolicy` 的那半句——它**确实被读、被记录，却【不改变任何判定】** ✗（直到本轮 ✓）。
  **并把 `priorityPolicy` 做成真判定** ✓✓：`fifo`（默认）＝先到先得、冲突即拒（**原行为零变化** ✓）；**`preempt`＝新预约拿到窗口，被挤者移出【活动集】但留在【台账】里**（带 `displaced:{by,at,reason}` ✓ ⇒ **可审计、且回执里点名被挤者** ✓✓）。**未知取值 ⇒ 回落 `fifo` 并在回执/拒里【自曝生效值】** ✓（**笔误不能静默改变行为** ✓）。
  **实测（判定确实变了的最强证据 ✓）** ✓：`fifo` ⇒ 第二、第三个都 `VMU_CONFLICT` ✓；`preempt` ⇒ 第二个 **ok** ＋ `preempted={id:'rs-1',by:'m1'}` ✓ ＋ **★ 第三个也 ok（窗口真的腾出来了）** ✓✓；`nonsense` ⇒ 回落且 `priorityPolicy:'fifo'` ✓。
  **数字** ✓：套件 **12 → 14 条全绿** ✓；**`TOTAL keys 301 → 304`** ✓、**`recovered 298 → 301`** ✓（三个键的**真默认值** `fifo`／`queue`／`sha256` 由 `s('k', <lit>)` 形态恢复 ✓）、**诚实 null 仍 3** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 147：`attachCapture` 名不副实（键被读、却从未"附加"）—— 补成真行为，并让引用【只能引用、且进指纹】** ✓✓
  **发现（同族第 N 例 ✗）** ✓：`use()` 里只有 `if (c.attachCapture) mark('vmu.instruments.attachCapture')` ✗ —— **键被读取、被标记为"已求值"，但"附加数据捕获"这个动作从未发生** ✗✓：台账记录是 `{id, unit, by, purpose, at, fingerprint}`，**没有任何捕获引用字段** ✗。⇒ **"mark 了它"不等于"它起了作用"** ✓（本项目一路在抓的形态 ✓）。
  **处置（两个键一起做成真行为 ✓）** ✓：
  **① `attachCapture`（默认 `true`）** ⇒ `use()` **必须给出它引用了哪个库对象**（或继承 `dataCaptureRef` 的默认 ✓）；**引用为空 ⇒ 具名拒** ✓✓，hint 同时给出两条出路 ✓（给 `capture`／声明默认／确认不需要就关掉该键 ✓）。**理由**：**一条没有指向数据的指针的使用记录，正是没人能审计的记录** ✓。
  **② `dataCaptureRef`（默认 `''`）** ⇒ **默认引用** ✓（pack 用它声明"捕获放在哪" ✓）；**移入 `WIRED_INSTRUMENT_KEYS`** ✓（它被读、且**改变判定** ✓）。
  ★ **纪律** ✓：**只引不复制** ✓ —— 记录里存的是**引用**（`capture` ✓），且**引用进指纹** ✓ ⇒ **换引用 ⇒ 指纹必须变** ✓（已断言 ✓✓，这就是"它真的在记录里"的证据 ✓）。
  **实测** ✓：默认缺引用 ⇒ `VMU_META_VALIDATION_FAILED`（消息含"缺数据捕获引用" ✓）／给引用 ⇒ ok 且 `capture="lib/x"` ✓／`attachCapture:false` ⇒ ok 且 `capture=null`（**不是空串、也不编造** ✓）／`dataCaptureRef:'lib/default'` ⇒ ok 且 `capture="lib/default"` ✓／**换引用 ⇒ 指纹不同** ✓。
  **并顺手修掉一次【我自己的空过风险】** ✓：套件里那条"确定性"测试比较两次 `use()` 的 JSON —— 新契约下**两侧都会被拒** ✗ ⇒ **两个相同的拒绝比较相等 ⇒ 断言会通过却什么都没证明** ✗✓ ⇒ **给它们补上引用后，它又重新比较两条真实记录** ✓✓（契约变更会让旧断言**偷偷作废**，这条值得记 ✓）。
  **数字** ✓：套件 **14 → 15 条全绿** ✓（既有 9 处调用按新契约补齐 ✓）；**`TOTAL keys 304 → 305`** ✓、**`recovered 301 → 302`** ✓、**诚实 null 仍 3** ✓；划分实测 **`wired=15 planned=2 all=17 exact=true`** ✓（余下 `ledgerDir`／`downtimePolicy` ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 148：`ledgerDir` 落地 —— 注入式写接缝 ＋【写成功才发布内存】（把外部评审在 v5 上教的那一条用到仪器面）** ✓✓
  **做法** ✓✓：`createInstruments({ …, writeLedger })` 新增**注入接缝** ✓（**内核绝不自己碰宿主 FS** ✓，docs/11 §4.1 ✓）；`cfg()` 读 **`ledgerDir`** ✓（默认 `''`＝**只在内存** ⇒ **既有行为零变化** ✓）。**未接目录时接缝根本不被调用** ✓。
  ★ **顺序纪律** ✓✓：**先落盘、再进内存** ✓ —— **写失败绝不推进内存** ✓（这正是轮 139 从 PR #19 学到的 v5 教训：**失败的写不得让内存前进** ✓）。
  **实测（5 种情形 ✓）**：无目录 ⇒ ok／`persisted=null`／内存 **1** ✓；**有目录、无接缝 ⇒ `VMU_ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE`** ✓（具名拒 ✓）／内存 **0** ✓✓；**接缝抛错 ⇒ `VMU_IO_FAILED`／内存 0** ✓✓；**接缝返回 `ok:false` ⇒ 同等对待／内存 0** ✓✓；接缝成功 ⇒ ok／`persisted='D:/_tmp/instr-ledger/ur-1.json'` ✓／内存 **1** ✓，且**落盘文本含记录 id 与捕获引用** ✓（**引用随记录走** ✓）。
  **键表** ✓：`ledgerDir` **移入 WIRED** ✓（被读且**改变判定** ✓）⇒ 划分实测 **`wired=16 planned=1 all=17 exact=true`** ✓（**只剩 `downtimePolicy`** ✓，其理由是"需与运维/告警面协同" ✓）。
  **数字** ✓：套件 **15 → 16 条全绿** ✓；**`TOTAL keys 305 → 306`** ✓、**`recovered 302 → 303`** ✓、**诚实 null 仍 3** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 149：`downtimePolicy` 落地 —— 仪器面【17/17 全接线、零计划键】，并补上一个真实缺口** ✓✓
  **缺口（本轮之前真存在 ✗）** ✓：**`use()` 根本不看维护窗口** ✗ —— **一台已排入维护的仪器照样可以被"使用"** ✓✗（只有 `reserve()` 会检查 ✓）。这就是 `downtimePolicy` 该管的事 ✓。
  **做法** ✓✓：`cfg()` 读该键（**未知取值回落 `refuse`** ✓，**生效值随回执/拒一起走** ✓；**笔误不能把"拒"变成"许可"** ✓）。`use()` 里判定：**"现在"落在该台仪器的任一维护窗口内 ⇒ 机器处于停机** ✓；**`refuse`（默认）⇒ 具名拒 `VMU_NOT_PERMITTED` 并点名窗口与生效策略** ✓；**`degrade` ⇒ 放行，但把例外【记录在案】**（`degraded:{reason,from,to}` ✓，**进记录、进指纹、并出现在回执里** ✓✓）—— **例外是被记录的，不是被忽略的** ✓。
  **检查顺序（刻意定下 ✓）** ✓：**机器状态先于操作者手续**（培训／校准 ✓），与 paper 面"尺寸先于归属"同一条思路 ✓ —— **一台停机的仪器，无论操作者准备得多好都不能用** ✓。
  **实测** ✓：默认 `refuse`＋窗口内 ⇒ `VMU_NOT_PERMITTED`（带 `window` 与 `downtimePolicy` ✓）／窗口**外** ⇒ ok ✓（**边界就是窗口本身** ✓）／`degrade`＋窗口内 ⇒ ok 且 `degraded.reason='maintenance-window'` ✓／`nonsense` ⇒ 回落 `refuse` ✓。
  **数字** ✓：套件 **16 → 17 条全绿** ✓；划分实测 **`wired=17 planned=0 all=17 exact=true disjoint=true`** ✓✓；**`TOTAL keys 306 → 307`** ✓、**`recovered 303 → 304`** ✓、**诚实 null 仍 3** ✓。
  **过程失误（记下 ✓）** ✗：本轮我的编辑**撞上轮 146 留下的注释块** ⇒ 多出一个悬空 `])` ⇒ 语法检查当场报 `Unexpected token ']'` ✗；**并且我的探针又错了一次** ✗✓（`openWindow` 要 `from`/`to`，我传了 `hours` ⇒ 窗口根本没建 ⇒ 四种情形全"通过"而什么也没测到 ✓ —— **"全绿"当时是假的** ✓）。两条都由"先 `node --check` ＋ 逐情形实测"拦下 ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 150：一句我反复引用的数字是错的 —— 计划键积压实测为 19 条（不是"约 40"），并把它变成【生成物＋自检】** ✓✓
  **错在哪** ✗✓：多轮报告里我写"**约 40 个计划键未实现**" ✗ —— **实测（遍历 72 个模块的 `PLANNED_*` 导出 ✓）真值是：`6 面／19 条`** ✓：`storepolicy=8`／`ballotbox=6`／`course=3`／`compliance=1`／`paper=1`／`instruments=0` ✓。**根因** ✓：我把**两本不同的账**混为一谈 ✗ —— docs 里的 **666** 是**设计期声明账**（`settings/planned.js` ✓，生成物 ✓），而**模块侧"声明未接"账**是这 19 条 ✓。**两本账口径不同、数字差两个数量级** ⇒ **必须分开命名** ✓✓。
  **处置①（数字改为生成 ✓）**：矩阵生成器每次运行都打印 **`planned backlog (measured, module-side): 6 face(s), 19 entries :: …`** ✓ —— 从**模块自己身上**数出来 ✓，**不再由我叙述** ✓。
  **处置②（★ 手写键数直接判红 ✗✓）**：`EXPECT` 的 note 里**不许再写键数** ✓ —— 新自检当场抓到 **2 条**：`instruments` 仍写"**17 键中 14 已接／3 未接**" ✗（**该面本轮已是 17/0** ✓），`compliance` 写"**16 wired ＋ 1 planned**" ✗ ⇒ ★ **这正是我在轮 146 写下的教训（一次更正必须更新所有重复该说法的地方）在同一个文件里再次发生** ✓。
  **处置③（自检本身也要够宽 ✓）** ✗✓：第一版正则只认"`N 已接`" ✗ ⇒ **漏掉** `12/12 键已接`／`11/11`／`16/16`／`15/15` 这类**同样会漂**的写法 ✓ ⇒ **收紧后**又多清掉 **5 条**（`capacity`／`hr`／`funding`／`storepolicy`／`migration` ✓）⇒ **FINDING 归零** ✓。
  **过程失误（记下 ✓）** ✗：清理脚本第一版**自身语法错**（全角 `（` 配半角 `)` ✗）⇒ **它在改任何东西之前就中止了** ✓ —— **失败关闭（fail closed）**，没留下半成品 ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 151：`storepolicy` 八条"未读取该键"【全部是错的】—— 改用"两值对比"实测，本面清零** ✓✓
  **发现** ✗✓：计划表写着八条"**未读取该键**" ✗ —— **`cfg()` 八条全读** ✓（L79-93 ✓）。★ **但"被读"不等于"决定什么"** ✓ ⇒ 于是做**可证伪的效果测试** ✓：每条键用**两个值**驱动**同一次调用**，比较**结果** ✓✓（读源码只能提示，比较才是证据 ✓）。
  **实测** ✓✓：**7 条当场改变结果** ✓ —— `backend`（未知值具名拒 ✓）、`root`（`file` 后端必需 ✓）、`tmpDir`（必须落在 root 下 ✓）、`remote`（`on` 必须有 url ✓）、`remote.url` ✓、`remote.offlinePolicy`（拒 vs 排队 ✓）、`onVersionTooHigh`（拒 vs warn ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 157：最后一个确认缺口 `irvInstantSingleCount` 落地 —— 多轮淘汰计票，且【实现与测试同轮】** ✓✓
  **落点＝轮 152 写下的落点** ✓：**`ballot.js` 的 `defaultTally` 旁**（★ **唯一计票真源** ✓；且它本就是**可注入接缝**（`tally` 参数 ✓）⇒ 选它**不必改内核形状** ✓）。
  **IRV 计票器** ✓（`instantRunoffTally` ✓）：**续存票的严格过半即当选** ✓；否则**淘汰首轮票最少者，并把那些票【转移】** ✓（IRV 的定义性质 ✓）；★ **票面耗尽（所排选项全被淘汰）⇒ 停止计数并【报告耗尽数】** ✓✓（**静默丢弃会改变谁当选** ✗）；★★ **末位并列不由它裁定** ✓✓ —— 返回 `undecided` 并**点名并列集合** ✓（**平票规则归 pack** ✓，**绝不隐含排序** ✗）。**结果携带 `rounds[]`** ✓ ⇒ **淘汰路径可审计，而不只是"谁赢"** ✓。
  **接线** ✓：`ballotbox` 的 `method` 白名单增 **`irv`** ✓；★ **`irvInstantSingleCount` 的语义**＝**IRV 必须在【一次裁定】内解完所有淘汰轮** ✓（轮次模型无法表达 IRV：那要**每一轮重开一次会议** ✗）⇒ **关着时 `irv` 具名拒 `VMU_BALLOT_METHOD_UNSUPPORTED`** ✓，**开着时一次解完并把 `rounds[]` 全程回执** ✓；`cast` 新增 `choices`（排序表 ✓）而 **`choice` 语义一字未改** ✓（既有方法零变化 ✓）。
  **数字** ✓：划分 **`wired=22 planned=5 all=27`** ✓ ⇒ **积压 9 → 8 条** ✓（余下五条均由别面承载并附证据 ✓）。
  **过程失误（三次都是我的 ✗，且都靠"打印"而非"猜"定位）** ✗✓：① **转移用例设计错了** ✗ —— 它让某选项**首轮就过半** ⇒ **根本不需要转移**；**计票器是对的，期望是错的** ✓（改为 `p=2 r=2 s=1`：淘汰 `s`，**它的票在第二轮把 `p` 选上** ✓）；② **ballotbox 的测试块插到了 runner【之后】** ✗ ⇒ **惰性、根本没跑** ✓（正是轮 151 记下的坑 ✓）；③ **我的收尾脚本自身语法错** ✗（模板字符串里含反引号 ✓）⇒ **它在改任何文件之前就中止** ✓✓ ⇒ 改成**片段文件拼接** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 157 的【未决发现】（→ 轮 158 已修复 ✓✓，见上条）** ✓：**盒面 IRV 回执里 `rounds` 与 `tie` 都是空的** ✗✓ —— **两个症状同一根因** ✓：**计票器走了"没有任何排序票"的早退分支** ✓（`rounds: []` ✓ 且 `tie: []` ✓ 正是那一条的唯一返回形态 ✓）⇒ ★ **是我接线里的真 bug** ✓（**盒面 IRV 分支尚未真正工作** ✓），**不是"期望写错"** ✗。
  **处置** ✓✓：**撤下那 5 条盒面断言** ✓（**把 `[]` 写成期望＝把 bug 变成规格** ✗✗）；**保留已通过的两条**（关着时具名拒 `VMU_BALLOT_METHOD_UNSUPPORTED` ✓、开着时**确实走到裁定**并返回 `undecided` ✓）；**计票器本身的 6 条单元断言全绿** ✓（`VMU BALLOT: 41/0` ✓，含转移、并列点名、耗尽、非决定性票不计 ✓）⇒ **已验证的边界因此很清楚：计票器是对的，接线是坏的** ✓。
  **下一轮第一件事** ✓：定位盒面为何交出**空排序**（`cast` 存的是 `choices` 数组 ✓、`close` 读的是 `box.votes` ✓ —— 二者之间的某一步丢了排序 ✓）。
  ★★ **这条发现只在【把实测值打印出来】之后才出现** ✓✓ —— 我先前**两轮都在改"我以为的期望"** ✗，直到打出实际值才看见**代码本身的问题** ✓：**"期望错了"与"代码错了"在症状上一模一样，只有实测能分开** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 158：轮 157 那条"未决发现"【已修复】—— 并且它揭出一种更隐蔽的形态** ✓✓
  **根因（打印得到，不是猜的 ✓）**：`cast()` 里 **`box.options.indexOf(choice)` 被传进了【数组】** ✗ ⇒ `indexOf` 对数组**恒为 -1** ✗ ⇒ **每一次排序投票都被当作"选项不在票面上"拒掉** ✓ ⇒ **`box.votes` 因此为空** ✓ ⇒ **回执的 `rounds: []` 与 `tie: []` 完全吻合** ✓✓（**两个症状同一根因** ✓ —— 我上一轮就是靠"两个症状同时为空"把它推到"计票器早退"这一步的 ✓）。**第二处** ✓：`box.votes.set(by, String(choice))` ✗ 即便过了守卫也会**把排序字符串化毁掉** ✓。
  ★★ **形态更隐蔽的地方** ✓：**输入校验（守卫）与存储（落库）都被"标量假设"污染** ✗ —— **新参数的校验没跟着新参数一起改** ✓。这正是我这两轮反复遇到的"**加了分支，却只改了一半**" ✓（轮 155 的 `gate()` 要求一份无法产出的记录 ✓、轮 156 的 `review()` 缺参数 ✓，现在是"**守卫与落库只认标量**" ✓）。
  **修法** ✓：排序路径**逐项**校验并对**不在票面上的选项点名** ✓；**存储按数组落库** ✓；★ **标量路径一字未改** ✓（`indexOf(choice)` 与 `String(choice)` 原样保留 ✓ ⇒ **既有方法零变化** ✓）。
  **实测（修复后 ✓）** ✓✓：4 票例 ⇒ `undecided`（`b`/`c` 末位并列 ✓）、`rounds` 长度 **1** ✓、`counts={a:2,b:1,c:1}` ✓、`exh:0` ✓、`enforced` 含该键 ✓；2 选项并列 ⇒ **`tie=["x","y"]`** ✓；明确过半 ⇒ **`a`** ✓。
  **断言恢复** ✓：轮 157 撤下的 5 条**按实测值**全部恢复 ✓（★ **先测后写** ✓ —— 撤下时是"不把 bug 写成规格" ✓，恢复时是"不把希望写成断言" ✓）；`tied.tie` 也按实测恢复为 `['x','y']` ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 159：把"半改分支"家族做成【机械可查的审计】—— 它当场抓出一处，并已修好** ✓✓
  **动机** ✓：同一家族已在**连续四轮**里以**四种外衣**出现（轮 155 闸要求一份**无法产出**的记录 ✓／轮 156 `review()` **没有该参数** ✓／轮 157-158 **守卫与落库只认标量** ✓）⇒ **值得做成检查，而不是每次靠运气撞见** ✓。
  **工具** ✓✓：`scripts/audit-enum-branches.mjs` ✓ —— 规则**刻意机械、无法辩驳** ✓：凡"**白名单式枚举**"（`const allowed = [...]` 且随后 `allowed.indexOf(x) === -1` 把关 ✓），其中**每个值都必须在同一文件里【别处也出现】**（分支／默认／消息 ✓）；**只在白名单里出现的值 ⇒ 就是"声称支持、实际按默认走"** ✓✓（**出现次数计数** ✓）。
  ★★ **它不是空过** ✓✓：全仓扫到 **2 张白名单／6 个值** ✓，其中 **`plurality`／`runoff`／`irv`／`file`／`memory` 五个都有分支** ✓（**反例证明检查能区分** ✓），**只抓到 1 处** ✓。
  **抓到的那个** ✗✓：`ballotbox` 的 `allowed = ['plurality','approval','runoff','irv']` ✓ —— ★ **`'approval'` 从未在任何分支里出现过** ✓ ⇒ **一个 `method:'approval'` 的票箱会被【静默按 plurality 计票】** ✗✓（**声明支持某方法，却做另一件事，且无人被告知** ✓ —— 正是这个家族最纯的形态 ✓）。
  **修法** ✓✓：**实现 approval 投票**（不是把白名单删掉了事 ✓）：`cast` 接受 **`approvals`**（集合 ✓，且**标量 `choice` ＝ 单元素集合** ⇒ **旧形状零变化** ✓）；`close` 的 approval 分支按"**获准票最多者当选**" ✓；★★ **最多票并列【不由它裁定】** ✓（返回 `undecided` 并**点名并列集合** ✓ —— **与 IRV 计票器同一条规则** ✓，**平票归 pack** ✓）。非法项**逐项点名拒** ✓（沿用轮 158 的形状 ✓）。
  **实测** ✓✓：并列例（`{a,b}`／`{a}`／`{b}`）⇒ **`undecided`** ＋ reason `tie for most approvals` ＋ **`tie=["a","b"]`** ✓✓；多数例 ⇒ **`a`**（`{a:2,b:1,c:1}` ✓）；**标量例 ⇒ `a`** ✓（旧形状 ✓）；含不在票面选项 ⇒ **cast 被 `VMU_CONFLICT` 拒** ✓；★ **审计重跑：`0 finding(s)`** ✓✓。
  **数字** ✓：`VMU BALLOTBOX 13/0` ✓ 全绿。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**尚未把这个审计接进门禁** ✗ —— `tests/run-tests.mjs` 的**作业发现规则我还没核实** ✓ ⇒ **按"打印不猜"的规矩，接线留到下一轮** ✓（先读 runner 如何发现作业 ✓，再决定放 `tests/` 还是 `scripts/` ✓）；**同时应当把同一思路推广** ✓：**"新增枚举值/新增域/新增参数"时，校验、存储、回执、键表四处必须同步** ✓ —— 本轮的检查只覆盖**第一处（白名单）** ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 160：审计【接进门禁】—— 并且是靠读规则而不是猜** ✓✓
  **先读规则** ✓（**不猜** ✓ —— 前五轮的缺陷都源于猜 ✓）：runner 的收集规则写在它自己的文件头（L12-14 ✓）：**`tests/` 下每个 `.mjs`，凡不是 `*.test.mjs` 的，就是"探针"** ✓ ⇒ ★ **要让一个检查成为门禁作业，正确做法是把它放进 `tests/`** ✓（**不是**去某个列表里登记 ✗）。
  **处置** ✓✓：审计脚本 **`scripts/` → `tests/`** ✓；并**加进"精选快速子集"** ✓（否则它只在全量跑 ✓）；`QUICK_EXPECTED_JOBS` **27 → 28** ✓ —— 该文件自己写着"**只在有意变更时改，并在提交里说明**" ✓ ⇒ 注释按房内风格写为 **`27 -> 28, deliberately`** ✓。
  **实测** ✓✓：`quick-scope self-check ok (**28 jobs**, no mutant family)` ✓；**T1 118/118** ✓；生成物随之更新（作业总数 212 → **213** ✓，`README`／`AUDIT-CHECKLIST`／`test-timing` ✓）。
  ★★ **顺带验证了一条纪律在【后台续跑】下同样成立** ✓✓：轮 157 那条**遗留后台作业**刚刚结束 ✓，它的 T1 是**红的** ✗ —— 而收尾脚本里的 **`if ($code -eq 0)` 守卫把它拦在提交之外** ✓ ⇒ **实测 HEAD 仍 `e690841`、远端一致、最近四次提交正是 156–159 轮** ✓✓（**门禁红绝不提交** ✓，**即使跑在没人看着的后台** ✓）。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：★ 本审计**只覆盖"四处同步点"里的第一处（白名单）** ✗ —— **校验／存储／回执／键表**其余三处**尚未机械检查** ✓，那是下一轮的目标 ✓。

- ★★★ **轮 161：把审计扩到第二处同步点的【尝试失败】—— 失败本身是结论，且值得写下来** ✓✓
  **想查什么** ✓：**"疑似列表的值被当成标量用"**（**校验／存储**那一处 ✓）—— 正是轮 157 真出过事的形状（`indexOf(数组)` 恒为 -1 ⇒ 排序投票全被拒 ⇒ 存储为空 ✓，轮 158 修好 ✓）。
  **尝试①（空过 ✗）**：只跟踪**直接**写成 `= Array.isArray(...)` 的变量 ✓ ⇒ **用"轮 157 的复刻代码"做反向对照 ⇒ 命中 0** ✗✓ —— ★ **它抓不到它要抓的东西** ✓（真实缺陷走的是**派生变量** `choice = ranked ? ranked : …` ✓）。
  **尝试②（噪声 ✗）**：给污点加**赋值传播** ✓ ⇒ **366 个受染名、13 条 finding，全部是误报** ✗（`auditchain` 的 `String(recomputed)` ✓、`workflow` 的 `String(kind)` ✓ 与列表毫无关系 ✓）。
  ★★ **结论（本轮最值钱的一条 ✓✓）**：**"不会红的检查"与"永远在响的检查"是同一个缺陷的两面** ✓ —— **两者作为门禁同样无用** ✓。⇒ ★ **决定【不发布】它** ✓：探针**在提交之前就删掉** ✓（**未提交 ⇒ 不留历史噪声** ✓），并把两次尝试连同对照记在此处 ✓（**防止下一个人"随手写个 linter"然后相信它** ✓）。
  ★ **替代方案：人工清单** ✓（**三点，各带历史实例为范例** ✓）：
  **① 新参数出现** ⇒ **校验（坏值怎么拒）／存储（怎么落库、会不会丢信息）／回执（调用者能否看见）／键表（是否登记）四处必须同步** ✓ —— **五轮里的五处缺陷全部属于没有同步** ✓（闸要求一份无法产出的记录 ✓／`review()` 没有该参数 ✓／`indexOf` 收到数组 ✓／`String(列表)` ✓／白名单里有值而分支里没有 ✓）。
  **② 新枚举值出现** ⇒ 白名单 ✓（★ **已由 `audit-enum-branches` 机械覆盖** ✓）＋ 分支 ✓ ＋ docs ✓。
  **③ 新增域／种别出现** ⇒ 域列表 ✓ + 默认包 ✓ + 项码 ✓ **三处同步** ✓（`domaingate` 的形状 ✓：`DOMAINS`／`DEFAULT_PACKS`／`ITEM_CODES` ✓）。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**②③ 与 ①的四点仍【没有机械覆盖】** ✗ —— 本轮已证明**在这一规模下机械规则要么空过要么噪声** ✓；**保留一个健全的机械检查（枚举白名单 ✓）＋ 一份明写的人工清单，比再塞一个不可信的检查更诚实** ✓✓。

- ★★★ **轮 166：抓到一处【错得干脆】的状态断言，同时发现一份【做对了的范本】—— 后者比前者更值钱** ✓✓

- ★★★ **轮 167：把三轮的结论落成机械检查 —— 检查器成立，但【它的前提不成立】，后者是更重要的发现** ✓✓
  **检查器** ✓✓（`tests/audit-doc-planned-claims.mjs` ✓，**带 `--selftest`** ✓）：**取所有 docs 里"某键未实现/未接线/planned"的句子** ✓，拿键名去查**生成的 `04` 表** ✓ ⇒ **若该表标 ✅ 已接线 ⇒ 文档在说生成物反对的话** ✓。★ 两侧**都是标识符与标记** ✓ ⇒ 这是**精确**的判据 ✓（**不是散文筛** ✓）。
  **自检通过** ✓：合成"wired 键被说成未实现" ⇒ 命中 ✓；"planned 键被说成未实现" ⇒ 不命中 ✓ ⇒ **证明它不会空过** ✓✓。
  **实跑** ✓：**121 条键声明被核对，命中 72 条** ✓（`17` 卷 57 ✓／`08` 卷 9 ✓／`07` 卷 4 ✓／`14` 卷 2 ✓）—— 形如"`17` 卷 §教学把 `vmu.course.cohortMax`／`submitMode`／`reviewRounds`／`rubricRequired`／`requireEvidence`／`retentionMs`／`publishToLibrary`／`visibility` 都写作未实现" ✗✓。

  **实测** ✓：**18 个面有 `WIRED_*` 键表** ✓，**其中只有 6 个面的套件含"每个 wired 键都必须真被读"的断言** ✓（如 `course` 有 ✓），**其余 12 个没有** ✗（`meetings`／`tasks`／`notify` 等 ✓）⇒ ★★ **生成表里的 ✅ 目前只意味着"某模块把它列为 wired"，【不等于真的被读】** ✓✓ —— **它比看上去弱** ✓。
  ⇒ **处置（本轮）** ✓✓：**检查器保留为【报告】、暂不入闸** ✗（**它现在红着 72 条，而前提未验证** ✓ —— **把一个前提未验的检查接进门禁，就是再造一个不可信的检查** ✗✗）；★ **72 条降级为【候选清单】** ✓（**不是判决** ✓）。
  **下一步（有明确顺序 ✓✓）**：**① 先给那 12 个面补上"每个 WIRED 键必须真被读"的套件断言** ✓（**测试侧、机械、可复用** ✓ —— 这也正是每一面自己的纪律 ✓）；**② 补完之后 ✅ 才真正等于"被读"** ✓ ⇒ **检查器的前提成立，才可以入闸** ✓✓。
  ★ **方法论的第三层** ✓：轮 165／166 学到"**查标识符可靠、查散文不可靠**" ✓；本轮再加一条 —— ★ **查标识符之前，要先确认"那个标识符的标记"本身是否被验证过** ✓✓（**否则精确的检查会精确地传递一个未验证的前提** ✓）。

- ★★★ **轮 168：给第一个面补上"每个 wired 键必须真被读"的断言 —— 并且用【对照】证明这条断言本身能红** ✓✓
  **背景** ✓（轮 167）：生成表的 ✅ 只意味着"**被列为 wired**" ✗，**18 个面里只有 6 个**用套件断言了"**真的被读**" ✓ ⇒ **本轮开始补** ✓（**工作清单 12 个面** ✓：`capacity／compliance／conference／external／funding／grant／hr／ip／mathtools／meetings／migration／workflow` ✓）。
  ★ **范本（可复制到其余 11 个面 ✓）** ✓✓：在套件的**构造助手**处装一个**记录 getter** ✓（`const rec = (vals) => ({ get: (k) => { READS.push(k); return (vals||{})[k] } })` ✓），末了断言 `WIRED_KEYS.filter((k) => READS.indexOf(k) === -1).length === 0` ✓。★ **它成立的原因是被测模块【优先用 `settings.get`】** ✓（`meetings.js` L84 实测 ✓）；**若某面是直接读对象** ✓ ⇒ **等价的装置是 `Proxy`** ✓（`get` trap ✓，`hasOwnProperty` 与 `s[k]` 都会经过它 ✓）。
  ★★ **对照（本轮最该记的一步 ✓✓）**：**第一次跑就是全绿** ✓ —— **但那是【不可结论】的** ✗✗：该套件的 `ok()` **只在失败时输出** ✓，而"**断言插到了 runner 之后 ⇒ 惰性**"**正是轮 157 踩过的坑** ✓。⇒ **把断言临时改成必然为假** ✓ ⇒ 报红 ✓ 且消息显示 **`6251 reads recorded`** ✓✓ ⇒ ★ **证明块确实执行、记录器有效** ✓；随后从该消息的分支可见 `unread` **为空** ✓ ⇒ **该面 47 个 wired 键确实全被读过** ✓✓ —— **头部那句"为每个 wired 键有正反断言"的声明，现在从"声称"变成"被证明"** ✓✓。
  ★ **方法论第四层** ✓：**"全绿"本身也必须被证明【能红】** ✓ —— 前几轮在**检查器**上做过对照（轮 161／162 ✓），本轮把它用在**套件断言**上 ✓✓：**一个从未红过的断言，与一个不能红的断言，在输出里长得一样** ✓。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**其余 11 个面尚未补** ✗（**范本已定 ✓、每个面的装置形态已说明 ✓**）；**73 条候选仍未判决** ✗（**要等 12 个面都补完 ✓**）。

- ★★★ **轮 169：第二个面补上"wired 键必须真被读"，并踩中一个【新陷阱】：断言被放进了提前成功分支里** ✗✓
  **做法** ✓：`capacity` 面 ✓ ⇒ 在套件构造助手装**记录 getter** ✓（该模块**优先 `settings.get`** ✓，实测 L76 ✓）⇒ 末了断言 `WIRED_KEYS` 里没有被漏读的键 ✓；**对照** ✓ ⇒ 报红并显示 **`504 reads recorded`** ✓ ⇒ ★ **该面 wired 键全被读过** ✓✓。
  ★★ **新陷阱（值得单列 ✓✓）**：`capacity` 套件的尾部是 **`if (failed === 0) { 打印; exit(0) }`** ✗ —— 我第一次把断言块**插在了这个 `if` 的【内部】** ✓ ⇒ **条件已经判过** ✓ ⇒ 块里的失败**再也拦不住 `exit(0)`** ✗ ⇒ **对照跑了却"全绿 exit 0"** ✓✓ ⇒ ★ **如果没做对照，我会以为这条断言"验证通过"** ✗✗。
  ⇒ **处置** ✓：**断言的插入位置**必须**在**任何提前退出**之前** ✓（并把这条**写进注释** ✓，免得下一个人重踩 ✓）；前移后对照立刻报红 ✓。
  ★★ **与轮 157 的"插到 runner 之后"是同一族** ✓✓：**"代码在文件里" ≠ "代码会执行"** ✓ —— **本轮把这条教训从"句法位置"扩展到"控制流位置"** ✓（`after the runner` ✓ → `inside the early-exit branch` ✓）。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**其余 10 个面未补** ✗（`hr`／`compliance` 已定装置形态：**它们直接读 settings 对象 ⇒ 用 `Proxy`** ✓ —— `capacity` 的 get 记录器对它们**无效** ✗，这是本轮量出来的差别 ✓）；**73 条候选仍未判决** ✗。

- ★★★ **轮 170：`Proxy` 装置补上第三个面 —— 并当场抓到一个【真实的覆盖缺口】** ✓✓
  **装置差别（实测 ✓）** ✓：`hr.js` **直接读 settings 对象**（`hasOwnProperty` ✓）⇒ `capacity`／`meetings` 用的**记录 getter 对它无效** ✗（我上一轮量出并写下 ✓，本轮照做 ✓）⇒ 改用 **`Proxy`** ✓（`get` trap 覆盖 `s[k]` ✓ 与 `hasOwnProperty` ✓）✓；★ **并按轮 169 的教训【先读尾部】** ✓ —— `hr` 套件**没有提前退出分支** ✓ ⇒ 插在摘要前即可 ✓（`capacity` 需要相反位置 ✓，**所以每次都先读** ✓）。
  **对照** ✓ ⇒ 报红并给出未读清单 ✓ ⇒ ★★ **不空过** ✓。
  ★★ **抓到的真问题** ✓✓：**11 个 wired 键里有 6 个，在任何被测路径上都没被读到** ✗ —— `offboardingChecklist`／`performanceCadenceDays`／`recruitCycleDays`／`rotationPolicy`／`tenureDecisionWindowDays`／`tenureTrackMonths` ✓。**先排除了仪器可能** ✓：套件里 `createHr(` **只有一处**（就是 `mk` 自己 ✓）⇒ **没有绕过记录器** ✓；（`hr.js` 里这几个键**确实有读点** ✓，8–9 处 ✓）⇒ **结论：模块没问题，是它自己的套件无法让这些键生效** ✗✓ —— **这正是轮 167 那个假设（✅ 只代表"被列为 wired"）在真面上的首次实证** ✓✓。
  **处置** ✓✓：**不把缺口写成"全绿"** ✗，而是**具名且双向可证伪** ✓：断言要求未读集合**恰好等于**这 6 个 ✓ —— **多一个 ⇒ 红**（出现新漏洞 ✓）、**少一个 ⇒ 也红**（有人补了覆盖却没更新名单 ✓）✓。**这就是本项目"未做的必须明写未做"在测试侧的形态** ✓。
  **数字** ✓：`VMU HR 61 → 62/0` ✓；`VMU CAPACITY` 的**重复断言已清** ✓（68 → **67/0** ✓，外层那份仍有效 ✓）；**T1 118/118** ✓。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**其余 9 个面未补** ✗；★ **`hr` 的 6 个未读键【还未补覆盖】** ✗（**名单已具名 ⇒ 下一步是加能走到那些分支的场景** ✓，或**在 docs 里承认它们目前不生效** ✓）；**73 条候选仍未判决** ✗。

- ★★★ **轮 171：【撤回轮 170 的结论】—— 那 6 个"未被读"的键是【仪器假象】** ✓✓
  **怎么发现的** ✓：给 `hr` 加了四个能走到 `offboard()`／`rotation()`／`performance()`／`tenure()` 各分支的场景之后 ✓ —— **读取数【仍是 12】、未读集合【一个没少】** ✗✓ ⇒ ★ **这就说明问题不在被测对象，而在仪器** ✓。
  **根因** ✓✓：`hr.js` 的 `raw(s,k,d)` **先问 `hasOwnProperty(s,k)`** ✓ —— 而 **`hasOwnProperty` 走 `[[GetOwnProperty]]`，【不触发 `get` trap】** ✗ ⇒ 我传的是**空对象** ⇒ 成员检查为假 ⇒ **`s[k]` 根本不执行 ⇒ 一个读取都记不到** ✗✗（只有构造期的少数几次 `s[k]` 落了 12 条 ✓）。
  ⇒ ★★ **轮 170 那条"11 个 wired 键里 6 个在任何被测路径上都没被读到"是【错的】** ✗✓ ⇒ **本轮明确撤回** ✓✓，并把装置改成**三个 trap 都记录** ✓（`has` ✓、`getOwnPropertyDescriptor` ✓、`get` ✓）⇒ **实测 386 次读取、零未读** ✓ ⇒ `VMU HR: 62/0` ✓，**对照仍能红** ✓（**非空过** ✓）。
  ★ **这是"我的仪器错了"家族的又一例** ✓✓（此前有：PowerShell UTF-16 标尺 ✓、`TOOL_NAMES` 形状 ✓、`indexOf` 传数组 ✓、`openWindow` 参数 ✓、模板反引号 ✓、提前退出分支 ✓）⇒ 本项目**每条新装置都必须配对照**这条纪律，本轮再次证明是必要的 ✓✓。
  ★ **顺带保留的收获** ✓：为"补覆盖"而加的那 4 个场景**留着** ✓（无害 ✓，且**真的走到了 `offboard`／`rotation`／`performance`／`tenure` 的分支** ✓）⇒ **即使结论被撤回，覆盖本身是净增的** ✓。
  **教训（第三层之上再加一层 ✓）**：**"没看到"与"没发生"必须用两种独立装置交叉验证** ✓ —— 我此前在 docs/11 里写过"**sweep 摘录里看不到 ≠ 没有发生**" ✗✓（那时是日志 ✓），本轮**在测试装置上重犯了同一条** ✓✓。

- ★★★ **轮 172：把装置抽成【共享助手】，并用它补上第四个面 —— 通用装置同时覆盖两种 settings 形态** ✓✓
  **助手** ✓✓（`tests/helpers/settings-recorder.mjs` ✓）：★ **在【所有路由】上记录** ✓ —— `has` ✓、`getOwnPropertyDescriptor` ✓、属性 `get` ✓，**以及 `get` 这个【方法】本身** ✓（`get: (k) => { note(k); … }` ✓）⇒ **一个装置同时服务两种形态** ✓：**优先 `settings.get` 的面**（`meetings`／`capacity`／`ip` ✓）走**方法体** ✓；**直读对象的面**（`hr`／`compliance`／`storepolicy` ✓）走 **trap** ✓✓。
  ★ **为什么必须这样** ✓：助手文件头把**轮 170 那个教训**写在最前面 ✓ —— **只记 `get` trait 的 Proxy 对"先问 `hasOwnProperty`"的面【什么也看不到】** ✗（`[[GetOwnProperty]]` 根本不经过 `get` ✓）⇒ **一个纯属仪器的"6 键未读"结论被发布了一轮** ✓✓。**把教训写进装置本身** ✓，比写在文档里更难绕过 ✓。
  **第四个面** ✓：`compliance`（**17 键** ✓、**直读对象** ✓、**尾部无提前退出**（**先打印过** ✓））⇒ 装好装置后**对照触发** ✓ 并显示 **`551 reads recorded`** ✓、**无 `unread:` 前缀 ⇒ 未读为零** ✓✓ ⇒ **该面 wired 键全被读过** ✓；恢复真断言后 `VMU COMPLIANCE 63/0` ✓。
  **进度** ✓：**4 / 12** 面完成 ✓（`meetings` ✓、`capacity` ✓、`hr` ✓、`compliance` ✓），**每面都有对照证明断言能红** ✓。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：**其余 8 个面未补** ✗（`conference`／`external`／`funding`／`grant`／`ip`／`mathtools`／`migration`／`workflow` ✓ —— ★ **`ip` 已量明为"优先 `settings.get`"** ✓，**用同一助手即可** ✓）；**73 条候选仍未判决** ✗。

  **② `14` 卷【整卷退出该检查】** ✓（**有据** ✓）：它是**修正的活账本** ✓ ⇒ **按构造必然引用它所推翻的那些话** ✓（"`17` 卷曾把 `vmu.course.cohortMax` 称作未实现" ✓）⇒ **每一条都会被永久重报** ✗ ⇒ ★ **"修正的日志，不能被按它所修正的散文的标准来要求"** ✓✓。

  **下一批** ✓：`08` 卷（**9 条** ✓，规模合适 ✓）⇒ 然后 `17` 卷（57 条 ✓，分批 ✓）。

  **结论** ✓✓：**三次（轮 177／178／179）都是检查器错、散文对** —— ★ **这条经验已足够稳定**：**逐行启发式在"一行里同时讲两件事"的中文技术散文上必然过度触发** ✓ ⇒ **检查器只在"键是声明主语"时才报** ✓，**它现在的 0 候选是真实的** ✓（**A 对照证明它仍会报** ✓）。

  **怎么发现的** ✓：本轮按"约每 5–6 个 commit 跑一次全量"的纪律跑了全量 ✓ ⇒ **215 作业里 3 条失败** ✗：`v5-institute-fixes.mutants.mjs` **两片**（612.8s／576.4s ✓）＋ `audit-declaration-criterion.test.mjs`（**69/4** ✓）。

  ★ **性质** ✓：那是 **RULE E 指纹基线** 检查 ✓ —— 报的是 **"NEW vacuous pass `vmu-ab-v5r.test.mjs:107 [B]`"** ✗ 与 **"baseline entry disappeared (`vmu-capaci…`)"** ✗ ＋ **"A-class 指纹集必须与基线恰好一致（55 条）"** ✗ ⇒ ★ **它自己就写着处置办法：`sync the list explicitly if this is intentional`** ✓ ⇒ **下一轮第一件事：显式同步基线并写清原因** ✓。

  ★★ **纪律层面的教训（重要 ✓✓）**：**我一直遵守的"绿树"只在 `GATE_SCOPE=vmu`（T1 ＋ 精选审计）上验证过** ✗ ⇒ **全量范围的红因此能长期存在而无人发现** ✓✓ ⇒ ★ **"约每 5–6 commit 全量一次"不是可选的礼貌，而是唯一能看见这类红的机制** ✓；**本轮跑晚了，这是流程失职** ✗（**记下，不再重复** ✓）。

  **三件一起做（轮 194 的教训：单独做任何一件都会让门禁红 ✓）** ✓✓：

  ② **审计**：`audit-vmu-docs.test.mjs` 不再自己重算弱规则 ✓，★ **改为委托同一份 `hasConsumer`** ✓✓（**一条规则、一处实现** ✓）；★ **import 必须放在【文件顶部】** ✓（轮 194 放进代码块导致 `SyntaxError` ✗）。
  ③ **`M10` 家族**：它原本翻 `vmu.middleware.entries` 当作"已接线的行" ✗ —— ★ **在新规则下那个键只是被声明、从未被读** ✓ ⇒ **家族建立在【假前提】上，规则一修它就不红了** ✗✓ ⇒ ★ **样本换成 `vmu.prune.autoRun`** ✓（`settings['vmu.prune.autoRun']` 真读 ✓，`kernel/retention.js` ✓）；★ 并把断言从"必须匹配某一句"改为"**必须是具名红且输出里点名被翻的那一行**" ✓✓（**否则家族依赖检查顺序，太脆** ✓）。
  ★★ **结果** ✓✓：`docs/04` 的 **18 行自相矛盾 ⇒ 0** ✓；**已接线 750 → 731** ✓（**恰好少 19** ✓ ＝ 18 行 ＋ 1 处 `Symbol` ✓）；**`audit-vmu-docs.test` 70/0** ✓；**`audit-vmu-docs.mutants` 15/0** ✓；抽样 `cohortMax`／`gc.autoRun`／`retention.maxBytes` **仍为已接线** ✓，`maxUnits` 为 **`planned ＋ ⚠️ 未接线`** ✓✓。
  ★ **一处【写明的】限制** ✓：审计委托后，**接线是从【真实仓库】重算的** ✓，**不是**从家族复制出来的 `code` 副本 ✗ ⇒ ★ **若某家族靠改【代码】来改接线，就不再被看见** ✗ —— **本文件的接线家族全在 docs 侧** ✓，**所以可以接受** ✓，**但写在代码注释与账本里，不埋在沉默中** ✓✓。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：★ **修复过程暴露的"非 planned 却未接线"由 2 → 9 条未逐条定论** ✗（`vmu.core.storeBackend`／`vmu.safety.approvalRequired` 抽查为**真未接线** ✓；★ **`vmu.meetings.quorumCap`／`reconsiderFloor` 是否真被 packs 读** ✗ ⇒ **轮 179 的依据也只是"packs 里出现了名字"** ✗✓）；**v5 institute 两条 mutant 家族真因** ✗；**52 条冲突候选子集** ✗。
- ★★★★ **轮 196：查出 5 个"由 pack 供给、却【无人读取】"的 v5r 兼容设置 —— v5r 语义并未被 vmu 复现** ✗✓
  **判据** ✓（**用轮 195 刚建立的行级规则** ✓）：**数组元素行／对象键行＝声明** ✓；**索引读取／`readKey` ＝读取** ✓。逐条量这 5 个键（`quorumCap`／`reconsiderFloor`／`verdictMaxRounds`／`hardLimitMs`／`wakeRetries` ✓）：★ **在所有运行源里的出现【全是对象键形式】** ✓（`'vmu.meetings.quorumCap': 3,` ✓ —— **pack 的 settings 载荷** ✓），而 **`kernel/meetings.js` 对它们零命中** ✗ ⇒ ★★ **独立复核：非声明形态（即真读取）出现次数 = 0** ✓✓。
  ⇒ ★★ **结论** ✓✓：**packs 只是【供给数值】，没有任何运行源【读取】它们** ✗ ⇒ ★ **"供给" ≠ "消费"** ✓✓；★ 而且 **`docs/04` 已经如实标 `⚠️ 未接线（改了不会有行为变化）`** ✓（**轮 195 那处修复的直接效果** ✓）⇒ **错的是散文，不是表** ✓。

  ★★ **实质发现（比散文更正重要 ✓✓）**：★ **这 5 项都是 v5r 的行为参数** ✓（法定数上限 ✓／复议门槛下限 ✓／复算轮次上限 ✓／会议墙钟硬界 ✓／唤醒重试上限 ✓）⇒ **v5r 把它们交给 pack 会设成值，而 vmu 内核【不读】** ✗ ⇒ ★★ **即：v5r 的这 5 项语义在 vmu 侧【没有被复现】** ✗ —— ★ **这是 v5r 包含性的一条真缺口** ✓✓（**不是文档问题，是行为缺口** ✓）。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：★ **这 5 项语义是否要复现（以及在哪一面复现）未决** ✗（**本轮只判定"今天没有"** ✓，**不替它设计** ✓）；**v5 institute 两条 mutant 家族真因** ✗；**52 条冲突候选子集** ✗；**其余 4 条"非 planned 却未接线"未逐条定论** ✗。
- ★★★ **轮 197：实现 `vmu.meetings.wakeRetries`（5 项 v5r 行为参数的第一项）—— 成立但【差一个说明面】，按纪律回退** ✗✓
  **接口天然吻合** ✓✓：`wake()` 原本就有 `retry` 策略分支 ✓，但它**只返回 `retry: true`、没有任何上限** ✗，**把重试留给调用方** ✓ ⇒ ★ **`wakeRetries` 正是它缺的那个界** ✓；该键的契约（`docs/04` ✓）是"**同一成员同阶段的唤醒重试上限（钳制 [0,10]）；耗尽记 `unreached` 并视为『已获机会』**" ✓。
  **已实现（4 处内核改动 ＋ 4 条套件断言 ✓）** ✓：`cfg()` 读键并**钳制 [0,10]** ✓；加入 `WIRED_KEYS`（**47 → 48，故意** ✓，并按家规写明理由 ✓）；`wake()` 的 `retry` 分支改为**有界循环** ✓ —— **面自己拥有重试** ✓、**咨询时即 `mark` 该键** ✓、**耗尽则记 `unreached` ＋ `unreachedWakes` 计数** ✓，并返回 `retries`／`wakeRetries` ✓；`DEFAULTS` 与配置快照各补一行 ✓。
  ★★ **四条断言与对照** ✓✓：**上限耗尽 ⇒ `unreached` 且 `retries === N`** ✓；**`0` ⇒ 一次失败即 unreached** ✓；**第二次成功 ⇒ `woken` 且 `retries === 1`** ✓；**`99` ⇒ 钳制为 `10`** ✓。★ **反向对照**：**把读取改回常量 ⇒ 恰好这两条断言变红** ✓✓（**断言是承重的** ✓）。★★ 且**轮 168 那条"每个 wired 键必须真被读"通过了** ✓✓ —— **新增 wired 键会强制新场景真的去读它，纪律自我闭合** ✓。
  ✗ **卡住的一处（一步之遥 ✓）** ✗：套件自身的不变式 `status().keys reports a real value for all 48 wired keys (no silent nulls)` 仍失败 ✓ —— 说明 **`status().keys` 的值来自【另一个面】** ✗（不是 `DEFAULTS` ✓，也不是我改的配置快照 ✓）⇒ ★ **需要找到 `status().keys` 的构造源并补一行** ✓（**这是唯一未完成的动作** ✓）。
  **为何回退** ✓：T1 因此变红 ✗ ⇒ **"门禁红不提交"优先** ✓；★ 本轮预算已尽 ✓ ⇒ **回退内核与套件改动** ✓，**账本如实记为"已实现、已对照、差一个说明面"** ✓✓（**而不是把它说成做完了** ✓）。
  ✗ **一处自误（记下 ✓）**：初次改内核时**3 处锚点因缩进不符被跳过**（`cfg()` 里是 4 空格 ✓、`WIRED_KEYS` 是 2 空格 ✓，我写了 6／4 ✗）⇒ **只落地了 `wake()` 一处** ✓ ⇒ 现象是 **`K.wakeRetries` 为 `undefined`、`retries: 0`** ✓ —— ★ **"锚点必须逐字取自文件"，不是凭记忆重写** ✓✓。
  **未做（明写 ✗）**：★ **`status().keys` 的构造源未定位与补齐** ✗（**下一轮第一件事** ✓）；**其余 4 项 v5r 行为参数**（`quorumCap`／`reconsiderFloor`／`verdictMaxRounds`／`hardLimitMs` ✓）✗；**v5 institute 两条 mutant 家族真因** ✗。

  **补上轮 197 缺的那一环** ✓✓：`status().keys: keysSnapshot(K)` ✓ —— ★ 而 `keysSnapshot` 是一张**显式映射** ✓（其注释写着"**a name-derived lookup silently produced nulls**" ✓ ⇒ **必须手加一行** ✓）⇒ 加 `'vmu.meetings.wakeRetries': K.wakeRetries,` ✓ 后套件转绿 ✓✓。
  **完整改动** ✓（**内核 5 处 ＋ 套件 3 处 ＋ 1 个场景块 ✓**）：`cfg()` 读键并**钳制 [0,10]** ✓；加入 `WIRED_KEYS`（**47 → 48，故意** ✓）；`DEFAULTS` 补默认 ✓；**`keysSnapshot` 补一行** ✓；`wake()` 的 `retry` 分支改为**有界循环** ✓（**面自己拥有重试** ✓、**咨询即 `mark`** ✓、**耗尽记 `unreached` ＋ 计数** ✓）。

  ★ **纪律自我闭合的又一例** ✓✓：**新增 wired 键会迫使轮 168 那条"每个 wired 键必须真被读"的断言要求套件真的读它** ✓ —— **新场景确实读了，因此该断言通过** ✓✓。

  **落点与契约都对了** ✓✓：硬墙钟检查放进 **`speak()`** ✓ —— **正是 turns/tokens/wall 预算守卫所在处** ✓；★ 契约里那句不寻常的话被**逐字遵守** ✓：**"钳制 [300000, 7200000]；不存在『无界』"** ✓ ⇒ 读取写作 `Math.min(7200000, Math.max(300000, n(sget(…)) || 1800000))` ✓ —— **`0` 不再意味无限** ✓（**与软预算 `budgetWallMs` 的 `0`＝无限恰好互补** ✓✓）；错误码**复用 `VMU_RESOURCE_BUDGET`** ✓（"须给当前值与上限" ✓），拒绝消息带 `elapsed / limit` ✓。',

  '',- ★★★★ **轮 202：查出 `vmu.meetings.reconsiderFloor` 的模型【在本面无载体】并写进 schema 的唯一真源；同时修掉轮 199 遗留的 `|| 3` 歧义** ✓✓

- ★★★★★ **轮 219（超长区间）：货币族 11 条 ＋ 协议族 7 条一起做完；未接线 424 → 406** ✓✓
  ★★ **先纠正上一区间的一个错误结论** ✗✓：我在轮 218 写下「`vmu.funds.*` 在 `docs/22` 里**没有任何契约声明**」✗ —— **那是错的** ✓。实测：**该族 11 条全部有声明** ✓，就在 **`docs/22` 的统一参数表 L762-771** ✓（我上轮只查了 §3.1–3.4 分节与另一张表，**漏了那张表** ✗）。⇒ ★★ **教训：判定"无声明"之前，必须遍历所有声明形态（分节表、统一表、散列行），否则会把"我没找到"当成"不存在"** ✓✓。本次因此**先量出全局图** ✓：424 条未接线里 **51 个族有声明契约、212 条完全没有** ✓ —— 这才是"可做与不可做"的界线 ✓。
  **① 货币族 11 条 → `kernel/funding.js`** ✓（该面自述"owns the MONEY RULES" ✓）：`currencyDefault` ✓／`roundingMode`（**half-up／half-even／floor／ceil／trunc** ✓）／`scaleByCurrency`（**覆盖 ISO 表并自曝 `overridden`** ✓）／`fxSource`＋`fxSnapshotDir`＋`fxMaxAgeDays`（**离线优先：无带日期快照或超龄快照具名拒** ✓）／`allocationRemainderPolicy`（**largest-remainder／first-party／last-party，且分摊结果【必须加回总额】否则具名拒** ✓）／`rejectFloatAmounts`（**内核强制：默认拒浮点** ✓）／`maxAmountMinor` ✓／`allowNegative` ✓／`auditTupleFields`（**只可增不可删：缺字段具名拒并点名** ✓）。★ 关键是接进**既有的唯一金额收口 `moneyOr`** ✓ ⇒ **每个金额路径同时获得该族的约束** ✓。**冒烟实证** ✓：`allocate([1,1,1],100)=[34,33,33]` ✓、**half-even 2.5→2** ✓、**JPY 精度 0** ✓、**无快照换汇 ⇒ `VMU_FUNDING_FX_SNAPSHOT_REQUIRED`** ✓；**`VMU FUNDING: 92 passed, 0 failed`** ✓。
  **② 协议族 7 条 → `kernel/collab.js`** ✓（**协议正是那个面据以交换的东西** ✓）：`registryDir` ✓／`kinds`（**未声明种类在开协议时具名拒** ✓）／`unsignedPolicy`（**block＝「未签不得共享」，未签协议下的交换具名拒** ✓）／`signatureRefRequired`（**签署无引用具名拒** ✓）／`expiryWarnDays` ✓／`reviewCadenceDays`（★ **逾期复核【只 REPORT 并告警、不擅自升级为拒绝】** ✓ —— **该契约没给这个键强制力，自己发明一个就是越权** ✓）／`scopeEnforcement`（**越出协议范围具名拒** ✓）；新增 **`signAgreement`／`reviewAgreement`** ✓ ⇒ **`VMU COLLAB: 39 passed, 0 failed`** ✓。
  ★★ **发现并修掉一处"接线但无效"** ✓✓：读套件时注意到 **`rejectFloatAmounts=false` 时我的实现仍然拒** ✗ ⇒ **该键没有任何可观察差异** ✗ ⇒ ★★ 改为**关掉时按 `roundingMode` 舍入**（**绝不回到旧的静默归零** ✓）⇒ **正负断言都能区分** ✓。
  ✗ **一处自伤（第四次同型，促成方法改变 ✓）** ✗：脚本**又一次**在 `"` 界定符的字符串里写了 ASCII 引号 ✗ ⇒ **解析期即死、什么也没落地** ✓（套件当时显示的是**旧结果** ✓，**差点被误读为"改动生效但测试通过"** ✗✓）⇒ ★★★ **改法：把断言写成【独立片段文件】，由脚本读取后拼接** ✓✓ —— **脚本里再无嵌套转义，整类事故归零** ✓。

- ***** 304 (user clarification): the RETENTION criterion is relaxed and inverted - "if it MIGHT have a consumer anywhere in these directions, KEEP it".
  The directions: mathematics research / formalisation / computation and tools / agent communication and MEETING systems / inter-agent collaboration / workflow / control flow / archiving / review and publishing / agent self-organisation. Scope explicitly includes infrastructure, optional features, interfaces and plugins, tunable parameters, agent-executable commands, scripts and protocols.
  The test is POSSIBILITY, not proof, and the burden of proof is on DELETION. A demotion now requires BOTH that no possible consumer exists in any of those directions AND that it duplicates an existing mechanism - and it requires USER CONFIRMATION before code is withdrawn.
  Recorded in the goal text (revision 5) and in docs/23 section 0b.
- ***** 304 RE-CHECK of what I had changed, honestly reopened:
  1. instruments (17 keys, 373 lines) was REMOVED in round 302. Under the relaxed criterion it has a POSSIBLE consumer: (e) inter-agent collaboration and (j) agent self-organisation, because it carries capabilityTags / requiresTraining / train - i.e. a qualification gate for who may use a shared resource. PROPOSED FOR RESTORATION, pending the user decision. Git history holds it.
  2. biospecimen (7 keys, 200 lines) was REMOVED in round 301. Under the relaxed criterion it has a POSSIBLE consumer: (i) review and publishing, because subjectRefStyle / mappingIrreversible ARE an irreversible pseudonym mapping (what blind review needs), and (h) archiving, because a chain of custody is provenance and tamper-evidence. PROPOSED FOR RESTORATION, pending the user decision. Git history holds it.
  3. hr (11 keys) and course (24 keys) were marked for demotion but NOT removed; both are now RE-JUDGED AS KEEP (agent seat lifecycle; and teaching/training agents), and the demotion notes are withdrawn. The duplicate-review remark about course stays as a design note, not as a reason to demote.
  4. mentor / attendance / committees / consensus / consent (plans only) are re-judged as KEEP (meeting system; human-subjects consent).
  5. clinical (9) and animal (9) keep their demotion: for them no possible consumer exists in any of the ten directions - and even that demotion still needs user confirmation before any code is withdrawn.
- ***** 305 DECISION (user delegated the call, with two pointers: "qualification/permission control is definitely necessary" and "do not use such strange names - a name must fit what it actually is").
  KEEP BOTH capabilities, and RENAME them to what they actually are:
  1. the family formerly named instruments is, inside the kernel, the registration, qualification, authorisation, maintenance window and retirement of a SHARED RESOURCE - so its name should be resource, not instruments. Its capabilityTags / requiresTraining / train are exactly the "who is qualified and authorised to use this" gate the user calls necessary.
  2. the family formerly named biospecimen is an IRREVERSIBLE PSEUDONYM MAPPING (what blind review needs) plus a CHAIN OF CUSTODY (provenance and tamper-evidence) - so its name should be custody (with the pseudonym half expressed as anonymity), not biospecimen.
  3. clinical and animal keep their demotion: for them no possible consumer exists in any of the ten directions, and even that demotion awaits user confirmation.
  NAMING DISCIPLINE (user 2026-10-11, general): a name must fit what the thing actually is; "what it looks like" may not stand in for "what it is". Two existing counterexamples are instruments and biospecimen. Before naming a family, write one sentence saying what it actually is and take the name from that sentence.
  RESTORE MECHANICS: take the code and tests back FROM GIT HISTORY (do not rewrite them), restore under the original names first so T1 stays green, and do the rename as the immediately following, separate step - a rename touches about sixteen touch points per family and must follow the section 2.0 procedure. Until the rename is done, do not claim the name fits.
  NOT DONE YET (explicit): the restore itself. A reverse-apply of the two removal commits was attempted and ABORTED deliberately: it conflicted in six files and, worse, would have rolled back ledger and volume records written in rounds 301-304 (the ledger is a record and must not be swallowed by a revert). The restore will therefore be surgical: faces from 7eac256^ and bc29a5e^, their tests, and the schema/index/package/installer/doc rows re-inserted rather than reverted.
- ***** 305 RESTORE DONE: both families are back under their ORIGINAL names and the gate is green (130 passed, 0 failed, 107 suites).
  Proof: the kernel loads 105 services, where it loaded 103 after the removals, with instruments and biospecimen both true and every neighbour intact; the original three-family suite returned with the split file removed so the same assertions do not run twice; and the four audits are green (docs 72/0, cross-volume refs 773/0, package membership 12/0, installer policy ALL GREEN).
  Method, now reusable: decide per FILE between taking it wholesale and re-inserting only the family entries, by asking whether any commit touched that file AFTER the removal. Wholesale for the files with no later edits; targeted re-insertion for the two file lists and the docs/11 matrix rows; and RECORD files (the ledger, volume 23) are only ever appended to, never reverted.
  The tempting shortcut was refused deliberately: reverse-applying the two removal commits conflicted in six files and, worse, would have rolled back the ledger and volume records written in rounds 301-304. A record must not be swallowed by a revert.
  Renaming is NOT done: instruments should become resource (registration, qualification, authorisation, maintenance window, retirement of a shared resource) and biospecimen should become custody (chain of custody and provenance), with the pseudonym half expressed as anonymity. A rename touches about sixteen points per family and must follow section 2.0; until it is done, do not claim the names fit.
  Lesson recorded in docs/11 (entry 21): on Windows quote the revision argument when taking a file out of git history, because Node runs execSync through cmd.exe where a caret is an escape character - the failure reads as "path does not exist in <commit>" while the real cause is that the commit expression was altered.
- ***** 306 RENAME (instruments -> resource), the user rule being that a name must fit what a thing actually is.
  What it is: the registration, qualification, authorisation, maintenance window and retirement of a SHARED RESOURCE, plus the gate that asks who is qualified and authorised to use it. So the family is resource, not instruments.
  Identifiers renamed: the key family, the seven error codes, the factory, the three exported key lists (INSTRUMENT_KEYS / WIRED_ / PLANNED_), the module basename and path, the service registration name, and the suite filename. The suite is now tests/vmu-resource.test.mjs and passes 17/0.
  Deliberately NOT renamed: the many unrelated uses of the English word (an instrumented settings object, the honest instrument, an instrumentation artifact). Those are genuine and were listed before deciding, so the blind word replacement was refused.
  Still owed from round 304 and now written: volume 24 section G1 records the meeting A/B slice (14 passed, 0 failed; EQUAL 1 / PROPERTY 2 / NOT_ADOPTED 1 / DIVERGENT 1) and names the meeting happy path as the next slice.
  NOT done: biospecimen -> custody (with the pseudonym half as anonymity) is still only planned; the clinical and animal demotion still awaits user confirmation; and the real-machine re-run and version/release remain open.
- ***** 307 TWO RENAMES, both according to the user rule that a name must fit what a thing actually is.
  1. resource leftovers finished: the default ledger directory (was Ops/Instruments), the documented register() parameter, the planned tool name (was vibe_vmu_resource（⛔ 未实现）) - and, found while sweeping, a USER-VISIBLE Chinese error message that still said the resource was an instrument.
  2. biospecimen -> custody. The family is a CHAIN OF CUSTODY with provenance and tamper-evidence plus an IRREVERSIBLE PSEUDONYM MAPPING, so "biospecimen" named the wrong thing. Renamed: the key family, the six error codes (with SAMPLE_UNKNOWN becoming ENTRY_UNKNOWN and SUBJECT_REF_STYLE becoming PSEUDONYM_STYLE), the factory, the module, the service name, the exported key list, and the suite filename (it now reads vmu-custody-budget-audit because it covers custody plus budget plus audit).
  3. the pseudonym vocabulary was renamed for what it is: subjectRef became pseudonym and subjectRefStyle became pseudonymStyle. That is the vocabulary blind review needs, and calling it "subject" made it look like human-subjects research.
  4. proof: the kernel loads 105 services with custody true, biospecimen false, resource true and every neighbour intact; the settings table carries seven custody rows; the gate is green at 130 passed, 0 failed, 107 suites; and the four audits are green (docs 72/0, cross-volume refs 774/0, package membership 12/0, installer ALL GREEN).
  5. NOT done, and stated rather than implied: the BODIES of the two sections in volume 22 still describe the old, narrower framing (laboratory instruments; physical samples) and have not been rewritten for shared-resource qualification and custody. A note at the section head says exactly that, because a rename is not the same as a rewrite.
  Still open: clinical and animal keep their demotion pending user confirmation; the freezeThaw vocabulary inside the custody face is still named for physical samples and its rename implies a semantic reframe, so it is left for its own step; the real-machine re-run and version/release remain open.
- ***** 308 the two section bodies rewritten, and one self-inflicted bug found by the counters.
  Rewritten: the volumes now describe what the faces actually are. The resource section says a shared resource has an identity, an owner, capability tags and a maintenance window, and the people it serves are the resource administrator, the agents that use it and audit - not a facility manager and a principal investigator. The custody section says entries enter the ledger, move through a chain of custody and can be retrieved and returned, with an irreversible pseudonym mapping - not samples that are frozen and thawed. Six rows that listed obsolete planned error codes now point at the implemented code families instead of keeping dead names.
  Also renamed for the same reason, inside the custody face: freezeThawWarnAt became storageCycleWarnAt, freezeThawCycle became storageCycle, registerSample became registerEntry, sampleId became entryId, collectedAt became enteredAt, and the default ledger directory moved from Ops/Biosamples to Ops/Custody. The suite still passes with fifty-one assertions.
  THE BUG, found because a counter moved: substituting the bare word "sample" also hit two UNRELATED identifiers - vmu.metrics.sampleHighVolume and vmu.metrics.sampleRate became entryHighVolume and entryRate. The generator then re-registered the original keys, so one semantic had two rows and the unwired count rose from ninety-two to ninety-four. Everything looked green: syntax passed and the gate passed, and only the maturity audit exposed the difference. Fixed by restoring the two metric keys and regenerating; the count is back to ninety-two and the gate is green at a hundred and thirty with a hundred and seven suites.
  Lesson recorded as docs/11 entry 22: never substitute a bare word when renaming. Substitute qualified names, or enumerate every occurrence first and judge each one, and after any rename diff the GENERATED registries so an extra row reveals itself.
  Still open: the clinical and animal demotion awaits user confirmation; the real-machine re-run and version/release remain open.
- ***** 309 G2 first slice: v5r meeting-platform rulings compared one by one, using the FINAL ruling document as the authority.
  Compared: D1 through D10 plus D5a (eleven rulings) and K1 through K5 (the five key gaps) - sixteen items, and all sixteen have a carrier located on the framework side, each with a named piece of evidence (a method, a key, or a line of code).
  The strongest evidence is D3, the attendance and denominator semantics: the framework quorum function computes unanswered as roster minus present, NAMES the unanswered list, reports which denominator policy is in force, and its own comment cites the same ruling document section. That is not a similarity argument, it is the same rule stated in code.
  D10 is the second: the framework takes the idle action as a SETTING and has no path that closes a meeting on a timer, so "the framework never auto-adjourns" is structural rather than promised.
  Recorded honestly alongside: six sub-items need checking (the extension cap and preset exit under D4, the initiator tightening under D5, the outcome classification under D5a, the light-path gate under D7, the observer layering under D8, the three broadcast elements under D9), plus two candidate GAPS: the framework method table has no SECOND operation even though v5r makes seconding a first-class step, and whether the motion kind domain includes a procedural kind.
  Every "equivalent" claim stays open until an A/B assertion backs it, per section 0. NOT done and named: the remaining twenty-five GAPS items, the three hard clauses C1/C3/C5, the voting-method set, and the conflicts between the draft and the final volumes.
  Still open: the clinical and animal demotion awaits user confirmation; the meeting happy path under G1 is still uncompared; the real-machine re-run and version/release remain open.
- ***** 310 G2 second slice: the twenty-one GAPS rows and the three hard clauses, plus a correction of my own earlier verdict.
  The correction matters most. Last round I recorded "no seconding operation" as a candidate gap, on the strength of the meeting face method table. That was wrong: seconding lives in the neighbouring governance face, where the seconding threshold is a wired setting, the not-seconded refusal is implemented with its own named code, and the design volume already lists seconders, the threshold, the deadline and a seconded state. The claim was corrected in place rather than annotated, as the correction rule requires, and the lesson is now a development entry: a method table of one face is not the capability surface, so search the concept repository-wide before asserting absence, especially across neighbouring faces in the same domain.
  Of the twenty-one gap items and three hard clauses, thirteen have a carrier located with evidence and eleven carry a check mark. The two strongest are again shared with the first slice: the silence clause, where the framework already refuses to count an unanswered member in the denominator, names them instead, and defaults to that behaviour rather than offering it as an option; and the abstention item, which reuses the same denominator keys as the attendance ruling.
  The single most important thing left to check is named: the boundary between two kinds of aggregation - consensus convergence may average, procedural votes may not, and a procedural vote that misses its threshold must be recorded as undecided. If that boundary is not held, the consequence is exactly what the clause exists to prevent: the strength of a conclusion quietly inflated.
  Still open: the three drafting conflicts between the draft and final volumes, the voting-method set, the meeting happy path under G1, the twenty-five further tool families under G1, user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion, the real-machine re-run, and version and release.
- ***** 311 five of the eleven check items resolved, and the driver limit pinned down precisely.
  The most important one was the aggregation boundary: on the framework side the prohibition is STRUCTURAL rather than conditional. The tally is three-valued - all ones true, all zeros false, anything else undecided AND carrying a reason - process votes are asserted not to constitute a ruling, and there is no averaging channel in the ballot path at all: the word does not appear in the kernel meeting surfaces or the two volumes. That is stronger than the clause, which permits averaging in one place and forbids it in another.
  Four more located with wiring verified: ending a debate exists and refuses without a reason, with the code comment stating the exact rationale - an unexplained end is the silent-stop failure mode the rule exists to prevent - and a second end is refused rather than absorbed; the named-versus-secret ballot is a wired enum to be chosen before opening; procedural motion kinds are a wired list; and seconding plus appeal scope are wired.
  The driver limit is now recorded as a precise technical reason rather than an omission: a lightweight fake context CAN drive the tool bodies - all thirteen meeting tools register and answer - but it CANNOT drive the institute state machine, because that writes a state file and reads it back to verify, and a fake file system makes it report a stale file. So the meeting happy path remains uncompared not because nobody tried but because a trustworthy file-backed seam is missing. Both routes are named, and neither is chosen yet.
  Still open: six check items; the three drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; the happy path; the remaining tool families; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 312 THE SEAM WORKS: the state machine now loads and commits, and the first segment of the meeting happy path really ran.
  What the seam needs, measured rather than guessed: four members - resolve, stat (undefined when absent), readText, writeText - plus the context timeout hook the scheduler calls. A write that resolves with nothing counts as unknown-but-successful, which the code states explicitly.
  With that in place: starting the institute returns ok with four members, quorum three and voters four, and convening a meeting returns a real meeting id, the agenda, and a SPEAKING ORDER. So the previous round conclusion - that only tool bodies could be driven - has been pushed forward: a multi-step stateful flow ran for the first time.
  The remaining blocker is now located exactly, and it is architectural: the preset is actor-bound. The acting member is assigned only when the preset own scheduler starts a member turn, so convening works because it goes through the office path, while motions and every poll operation answer member-not-found for the office. That means driving motion, voting and tallying requires either a subagent harness that actually runs member turns, or the heavy driver with a real CLI and provider. Both routes are named and neither is built.
  So the uncompared meeting happy path now has a sharper reason than "nobody tried": the first segment IS compared, and the rest needs an execution-identity source a fixture cannot fake. The methodological lesson is recorded as a development entry: feasibility must be judged per operation and not per tool family, because within one family one operation goes through the office path and another demands a seated member.
  Still open: the A/B assertion for the segment that now runs (measured by prototype, not yet asserted in the gate); the six remaining check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; the remaining tool families; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 313 the seam is IN THE GATE, an over-strong claim of mine is corrected in place, and the A/B found a defect on our own side.
  Gated: the meeting A/B file gained six assertions that pin the state seam. The decisive one is not "did the call succeed" - without the seam the call also succeeds, and that is exactly why the earlier claim was wrong - but whether a state file is really written to disk: zero without the seam, a real .v5state.json with it. With the seam, starting the institute and convening a meeting (with its speaking order) are both asserted, and vmu queue order is compared against them as a property: both sides turn speaking rights into an ordered structure, in different representations.
  Corrected in place: last round I wrote that without the seam the institute state machine is undrivable. That was too strong. It runs; only its PERSISTENCE fails, the preset says so on stderr, and the failure does not fail the call. The seam governs persistence, not executability. The assertion was rewritten around the observable difference rather than the claim.
  The defect: the A/B refused to confirm a property I expected. v5r refuses a motion from a non-seated actor by name, and vmu refuses a SPEECH from a non-member - but vmu ACCEPTS a motion from a non-member. The meeting face is therefore internally inconsistent: membership is checked on speak and not on motion, against the rule that a non-member never votes. Recorded as DIVERGENT with its reason and a revival condition, and queued as its own work item: make motions refuse a non-member by name. This is what the A/B is for - not proving two codebases resemble each other, but catching our own face disagreeing with itself.
  Still open: fixing the motion gate (a kernel change, own interval); the rest of the happy path (motion, second, vote, tally) which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; the remaining tool families; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 314 THE DEFECT THE A/B FOUND IS FIXED, and the experiment that found it was first made rigorous.
  Rigour first: the round-313 comparison was not sound. It set a speech - which refuses when the speaker is not PRESENT - against a motion, while the membership helper returns true whenever no roster seam is supplied, which is the zero-mechanism design and not a bug. So the claim "the meeting face is internally inconsistent" had not actually been established. Re-measured with a roster seam that really rejects outsiders and swept across all sixteen operations, the picture is worse and simpler: four were gated (attend, speak, vote, leave) and ELEVEN were not - interrupt, queue, motion, fileAppeal, quote, verbatim, warn, expel, transferChair, recess, confirmMinutes. The three most serious were a chair transfer to an outsider, discipline aimed by an outsider, and minutes confirmation by an outsider.
  Fixed without changing any API: the nine operations that already carry a subject parameter now refuse a non-member BY NAME - the member for interrupt, queue, motion, fileAppeal and quote; by for confirming minutes; the member being disciplined for warn and expel; and the member taking the chair for transferChair, which closes the seizure path. Verified with the same sweep that found the defect: eleven of sixteen now refuse by name, and nothing broke - meetings suite 160 passed 0 failed, the A/B 25 passed 0 failed, the service surface 48 passed 0 failed, and the gate green at 130 with 107 suites.
  Named as not done: four operations have NO actor parameter at all - warn, expel and recess, where an outsider can still discipline or change the process because there is nowhere to write who is acting, and verbatim, which is a transcript read. Adding an actor is an API addition and needs a decision about when an acting member must be named; the inclination is recorded with it - require one whenever a roster seam is in force and leave the seam-less default alone, because mechanism should follow the seam. Both the decision and the implementation are left to the next interval.
  Also left: the rest of the happy path; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; the remaining tool families; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 315 THE RESIDUAL IS CLOSED: every operation that acts on or disciplines a member now requires a NAMED member actor.
  Implemented as decided last round, with the decision itself written into the code comment: with a roster seam in force, warn, expel and recess require the acting member to be named AND to be on the roster; without a seam nothing changes at all. That is the zero-mechanism rule - mechanism follows the seam - and it is why the 160 assertions of the meetings suite did not move: the conditional gate leaves a seam-less deployment exactly as it was.
  Measured, each in its own assertion: an outsider can no longer warn a member, expel a member, or suspend the meeting, and an UNNAMED actor under a roster seam is refused rather than silently allowed. The same sweep that found the defect now shows FIFTEEN of sixteen operations refusing a non-member, with verbatim the single exception.
  verbatim is classified rather than gated: it is a transcript READ, takes no subject, and is not a member act, so its access control belongs to the confidentiality family (vmu.meetings.confidentiality*) and not to the membership gate. Expressing it there is left as work, and saying so is the point - it is a different question, not a missing check.
  Still open: expressing transcript visibility in the confidentiality family; the rest of the happy path (motion, second, vote, tally) which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; the remaining tool families; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 316 THE READ SURFACES NOW HONOUR THE CONFIDENTIALITY LEVEL, which is the same species of gap one layer over.
  What was found: the meeting confidentiality level was recorded when the meeting opened, and it was already enforced on the quoting path, where the policy allow/redact/deny decides what a quote returns. But the two READ surfaces ignored it entirely: the transcript read consulted only whether capture was enabled, which is a recording switch and not a visibility rule, and the minutes read consulted no level at all.
  The fix puts the gate on the LEVEL rather than on membership, because reading a record is not the act of speaking: a restricted meeting may not be read anonymously once a roster seam is in force, and the actor must then be a named member. Everything else is untouched - a seam-less deployment, and the internal level, behave exactly as before. That is mechanism follows the seam again, and it is why the meetings suite did not move: 160 assertions still pass.
  Measured in its own assertions: a restricted read without an actor is refused with the confidentiality-denied code; the same read succeeds when a member is named; naming a non-member does not open it either; and without a seam, or at the internal level, reads are unchanged. The A/B file now holds thirty-five green assertions.
  Still open: the rest of the happy path (motion, second, vote, tally) which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; extending the A/B to the remaining v5r tool families beyond the meeting family; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 317 THE A/B LEAVES THE MEETING FAMILY: the task family and the member family are now compared too, and the comparison found a lost-update gap.
  Reachability and shape: v5r registers four task tools and five member tools, and each names its object - the subject on create, the task id on get, the purpose and the first task on hire, the id on fire. Our side has the same verbs: the task face creates, transitions and lists, and the member face hires, ends and assigns roles, driven for real in the assertion rather than read off a schema.
  THE FINDING: v5r REQUIRES expected_revision on a task update - the caller must state the revision it saw - while kernel/tasks.js has no revision concept at all. Two writers can therefore lose one another, and that is a real difference in behaviour, not a naming difference. It is recorded as DIVERGENT with its reason and revival: the face was built for a single writer, and the revision idea already exists elsewhere in the kernel (bid revisions, capacity staleness), so the shape is familiar; the revival is to give the task face an expected-revision precondition and refuse a stale write by name. That is a kernel change and gets its own interval.
  A closure worth noting: the member family comparison empirically supports the round-305 re-judgment that kept the hr family. Hiring and dismissing are first-class on the v5r side, each naming its reason, and our own member face both hires and ends, driven for real - so the two are the same mechanism under different names, which is exactly what the re-judgment predicted.
  The A/B file now holds forty-three green assertions across three families. Still open: the remaining v5r tool families (lean, record, lifecycle, papers, library, verification, messaging); the lost-update fix for tasks; the rest of the meeting happy path, which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 318 THE LOST-UPDATE GAP IS FIXED: the task face now carries a revision and honours an expected one.
  Design first, as the objective requires: the landing points were measured before anything was written - the design volume sections that hold the task API, its field schema and its parameter family; the factory style of the face, which takes explicit options rather than a settings object; and the exact column shape of the implemented-code table in the contracts volume.
  The mechanism has three parts, all tunable. Every task record carries a revision, starting at one and advancing with each accepted write. The mutating operations accept an expected revision, and stating a stale one is REFUSED BY NAME rather than merged. And a switch, vmu.tasks.requireExpectedRevision, defaulting to false, can demand that every write state the revision it saw.
  Measured behaviour, each case in its own assertion: with the switch off an undeclared write passes as before, a wrong revision is refused, the right revision passes and the counter advances; with the switch on an undeclared write is refused too, and the right revision passes. So the guarantee v5r gets by making the argument mandatory is available here as a switch plus an always-on stale check - the same protection, without breaking every single-writer caller.
  Named as not done: the advance and rollback operations have not been given the precondition yet, only the four mutating points that were read in full; and the three design rows in volume 08 are still owed, though the key is wired in the schema so the gate is unaffected.
  Still open: the remaining v5r tool families; the rest of the meeting happy path; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 319 THE DESIGN GOES BACK WHERE IT BELONGS, and one of last round "not done" items is corrected as mis-grouped.
  Written into the design volume: the revision arguments on the four mutating operations; the invariant that a stale write is refused by name and never merged, with the switch that can demand the argument; the revision field in the task schema; and a vmu.tasks row in the parameter-family table, which had none at all - not even for the stage list or the open-task ceiling that were already wired.
  The correction: advance and rollback were listed last round as mutating operations that still lacked the precondition. They are not task mutations at all - they move the RUN stage machine, which is a different object - so the revision precondition does not apply to them, and the claim was mis-grouped. Recorded in place, as the correction rule requires.
  What survived the correction is a real open question rather than a defect: two writers could advance the same stage machine, so a stage-level precondition of the same species is a legitimate possibility with a real consumer, and it is registered as such rather than dismissed.
  Still open: the stage-level precondition; the remaining v5r tool families (lean, record, lifecycle, papers, library, verification, messaging); the rest of the meeting happy path, which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 320 THE A/B TAKES TWO MORE FAMILIES: Lean (5 tools) and records (4), and both come out as PROPERTY.
  Reachability and shape: v5r names the file it compiles, the kind it archives and the name it reads back, and it exposes the background compile queue as a tool of its own. Our side turns out to have all three counterparts already: the Lean face carries the command, the asynchronous switch, the parallel job ceiling and the timeout, and it names in its own header the archive/read/list trio it mirrors; the computation face has the same queue discipline, where going over the cap means WAIT counted in queueWaitMs rather than a silent drop; and the records face is a kind plus a declared head-field set.
  So both sides run Lean asynchronously behind a bounded parallel queue, both keep a reusable Lean archive, and both insist that a recorded claim carry judgement. That is three properties measured rather than asserted, and the overall tally is now two equal, eight property, one not-adopted and two divergent over four families.
  The divergence in this slice is a design choice rather than a defect, and it is worth stating precisely: v5r HARD-CODES value, motive and probability as required arguments on every recorded proposition, method and sub-problem, while vmu requires whatever vmu.records.headFields declares. The framework does not know which judgements a given institute wants to demand, and hard-coding three of them is exactly the framework-decides-methodology move this design avoids. The revival condition is concrete and cheap: a pack declaring headFields to require those three restores the v5r behaviour verbatim - the mechanism is wired, only the policy is absent.
  Still open: the remaining v5r families (lifecycle, papers, library, verification, messaging); the stage-level precondition; the rest of the meeting happy path, which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 321 THE A/B REACHES SIX FAMILIES: verification and messaging are compared, and the two sides decide truth differently.
  v5r verification, measured: the academician opens a debate on a TARGET, every member casts a BOOLEAN verdict on it, and the debate is ended explicitly with a status before the aggregate runs. Truth is a consensus aggregate.
  vmu verification, measured: the arbitration face carries open, appoint, declareConflict, recuse, hear, rule, appeal and escalate. Truth is an appointed tribunal that must handle conflict declaration and recusal.
  So this is a real mechanism-level divergence and it is recorded with reasons rather than smoothed: v5r optimises for a small institute where consensus is cheap, vmu optimises for review-heavy work where a reviewer judging their own result is the natural conflict to guard against - which is the same reason the reviewer-conflicts family exists. Both nevertheless honour the rule that debate does not decide truth: v5r keeps ending the debate separate from speaking, and in vmu speak and say carry no ruling power at all. The revival is cheap and needs no kernel change - a deployment wanting the v5r semantics can drive votes through the ballot face and treat the aggregate as the verdict, which is a pack-level composition.
  Messaging, measured on both sides: v5r separates group chat, an office relay, a non-polling wait for the next change, a process-feedback library and a per-member self report; vmu has the chat and quoting surfaces inside its meeting face plus a library face for recorded material.
  The harness now holds sixty-seven green assertions across six families, with verdicts two equal, eight property, one not-adopted and three divergent.
  Still open: the lifecycle, papers, library and assign/prioritize families; the stage-level precondition; the rest of the meeting happy path, which needs a member-turn harness or the heavy driver; six check items; the drafting conflicts; the voting-method set; user confirmation for the clinical and animal demotion; the real-machine re-run; version and release.
- ***** 322 SEVENTH FAMILY: the lifecycle family and the paper/assignment family, and G5 - the set/configure question open since the first slice - is closed.
  G5 answered by measurement rather than by analogy: v5r set persists institute parameters AND can override each staff member LLM route, provider and model, with empty meaning inherit, inside its own state file; vmu has ZERO model or provider keys across all eighty-four kernel faces, and its set tool is a host tool. That is a design choice, and the reason is recorded: the framework has to be deployable against any harness, so binding an agent to a model inside the institute would hard-code one runtime into the design, while the host already owns that switch. The revival is cheap - once the host exposes a routing seam the key would be a thin declaration rather than a new mechanism - and under the relaxed retention rule it is registered as a possibility rather than dismissed.
  Two properties came out of the same slice. First, both sides separate PAUSE from STOP, and the semantics match: in-flight turns finish and no new wakes are issued, versus interrupting every member and releasing their sessions - though vmu makes the pause a setting with a scope, an idle action and degradation steps where v5r pauses wholesale. Second, both sides demand that work be assigned with a WHY and an ACCEPTANCE criterion, and that priorities be an ordered list carrying a reason.
